sarve vigata mṛtyavaḥ upāsanti mahātmānam sarve divya paricchadāḥ vimānaiḥ vividha ākāraiḥ bhrājamānaiḥ iva agnibhiḥ mahā indra vapuṣaḥ sarve vicitra aṅgada bāhavaḥ bhūṣita aṅgāḥ diteḥ putrāḥ tam upāsanta sarvaśas tasyām sabhāyām divyāyām asurāḥ parvata upamāḥ hiraṇya vapuṣaḥ sarve divākara sama prabhāḥ na śrutam na eva dṛṣṭam hi hiraṇyakaśipoḥ yathā sa dadarśa mṛgādhipaḥ sabhāyām sura cita ratna gavākṣa śobhitāyām kanaka vimala hāra bhūṣita aṅgam dititanayam sa mṛgādhipaḥ dadarśa Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca tatas dṛṣṭvā mahātmānam kālacakram iva āgatam hiraṇyakaśipoḥ putraḥ prahlādaḥ nāma vīryavān divyena cakṣuṣā siṃham apaśyat devam āgatam te dṛṣṭvā rukma śaila ābham apūrvām tanum āśritam vismitāḥ dānavāḥ sarve hiraṇyakaśipuḥ ca saḥ prahlādaḥ uvāca mahā bāho mahā rāja daityānām ādi saṃbhava na śrutam na ca no dṛṣṭam nārasiṃham idam vapuḥ daitya anta karaṇam ghoram manaḥ mama asya devāḥ śarīra sthāḥ sāgarāḥ saritaḥ ca yāḥ himavān pāriyātraḥ ca ye ca anye kulaparvatāḥ dhanadaḥ varuṇaḥ ca eva yamaḥ śakraḥ śacīpatiḥ marutaḥ deva gandharvāḥ ṛṣayaḥ ca tapodhanāḥ nāgāḥ yakṣāḥ piśācāḥ ca rākṣasāḥ bhīma vikramāḥ sthāvarāṇi ca sarvāṇi jaṅgamāni tathā eva ca bhavān ca sahitaḥ asmābhiḥ sarvaiḥ daitya gaṇaiḥ vṛtaḥ vimāna śata saṃkīrṇā tathā eva bhavataḥ sabhā sarvam tribhuvanam rājan loka dharmāḥ ca śāśvatāḥ prajāpatiḥ ca atra manuḥ mahātmā grahāḥ ca yogāḥ ca mahīruhāḥ ca utpāta kālaḥ ca dhṛtiḥ matiḥ ca ratiḥ ca satyam ca tapaḥ damaḥ ca sanatkumāraḥ ca mahā anubhāvaḥ viśve ca devāḥ ṛṣayaḥ ca sarve prahlādasya vacaḥ śrutvā hiraṇyakaśipuḥ prabhuḥ uvāca dānavān sarvān gaṇān ca sa gaṇa adhipaḥ mṛgendraḥ gṛhyatām eṣa apūrvām tanum āsthitaḥ yadi vā saṃśayaḥ kaścid vadhyatām vanagocaraḥ te dānava gaṇā sarve mṛgendram bhīma vikramam parikṣipantaḥ muditāḥ trāsayāmāsuḥ ojasā siṃha nādam vimucya atha narasiṃhaḥ mahā balaḥ babhañja tām sabhām divyām vyādita āsyaḥ iva antakaḥ sabhāyām bhajyamānāyām hiraṇyakaśipuḥ svayam cikṣepa astrāṇi siṃhasya roṣāt vyākula locanaḥ sarva astrāṇām atha jyeṣṭham daṇḍa mantram su dāruṇam kālacakram tathā aghoram viṣṇucakram tathā param vicitrām aśanīm ca eva śuṣka ārdram ca aśani dvayam raudram tathā ugram śūlam ca kaṅkālam musalam tathā mohanam śoṣaṇam ca eva saṃtāpana vilāpanam tathā apratihatām śaktim krauñcam astram tathā eva ca astram brahmaśiraḥ ca eva soma astram śiśiram tathā kampanam śātanam ca eva tvāṣṭram ca eva su bhairavam saṃvartanam mohanam ca tathā māyā dharam param prasvāpanam pramathanam vāruṇam ca astram uttamam astram pāśupatam ca eva yasya apratihatā gatiḥ nārāyaṇa astram aindram ca sārpam astram tathā adbhutam paiśācam astram ajitam śoṣa dam śāmanam tathā etāni astrāṇi divyāni hiraṇyakaśipuḥ tadā astraiḥ prajvalitaiḥ siṃham āvṛṇot asura uttamaḥ sa hi amarṣa anila udbhūtaḥ daityānām sainya sāgaraḥ kṣaṇena plāvayāmāsa mainākam iva sāgaraḥ prāsaiḥ pāśaiḥ ca khaḍgaiḥ ca gadābhiḥ musalaiḥ tathā śataghnībhiḥ ca dīptābhiḥ daṇḍaiḥ api su dāruṇaiḥ te dānavāḥ pāśa gṛhīta hastāḥ mahā indra tulya aśani vajra vegāḥ samantataḥ abhyudyata bāhu kāyāḥ sthitāḥ tri śīrṣāḥ iva nāgapāśāḥ suvarṇa mālā ākula bhūṣita aṅgāḥ pīta aṃśuka ābhoga vibhāvita aṅgāḥ muktā āvalī dāma sanātha kakṣāḥ haṃsāḥ iva ābhānti viśāla pakṣāḥ teṣām tu vāyu pratimā ojasām vai keyūra maulī valaya utkaṭānām tāni uttamāṅgāni abhitas vibhānti prabhāta sūrya aṃśu sama prabhāṇi giriḥ yathā saṃtata varṣibhiḥ ghanaiḥ kṛta andhakāra antara kaṃdaraḥ drumaiḥ na akampata ājau bhagavān pratāpa sthitaḥ prakṛtyā himavān iva acalaḥ saṃtrāsitāḥ tena nṛsiṃha rūpiṇā diteḥ sutāḥ pāvaka tulya tejasā bhayāt viceluḥ pavana uddhuta aṅgāḥ yathā ūrmayaḥ sāgara vāri saṃbhavāḥ Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca kharāḥ khara mukhāḥ ca eva makara āśīviṣa ānanāḥ īhāmṛga mukhāḥ ca anye varāha mukha saṃsthitāḥ bāla sūrya mukhāḥ ca anye dhūmaketu mukhāḥ tathā haṃsa kukkuṭa vaktrāḥ ca vyādita āsyāḥ bhaya āvahāḥ siṃha āsyāḥ lelihānāḥ ca kāka gṛdhra mukhāḥ tathā dvi jihvakāḥ vakra śīrṣāḥ tathā ulkāmukha saṃsthitāḥ mahā grāha mukhāḥ ca anye dānavāḥ bala darpitāḥ śaila saṃvarṣmaṇaḥ tasya śarīre śara vṛṣṭibhiḥ evam bhūyas aparān ghorān asṛjan dānava īśvarāḥ te dānava śarāḥ ghorāḥ dānava indra samīritāḥ tatas cakrāṇi divyāni daityāḥ krodha samanvitāḥ mṛgendrāya asṛjan āśu jvalitāni samantataḥ tāni sarvāṇi cakrāṇi mṛgendreṇa mahātmanā grastāni udīrṇāni tadā pāvaka arciḥ samāni vai tāni cakrāṇi vadane viśamānāni bhānti vai hiraṇyakaśipuḥ daityaḥ bhūyas prāsṛjat ūrjitām śaktim prajvalitām ghorām dhauta śastra taḍit prabhām tām āpatantīm samprekṣya mṛgendraḥ śaktim ujjvalām huṃkāreṇa eva raudreṇa babhañja bhagavān tadā rarāja bhagnā sā śaktiḥ mṛgendreṇa mahī tale nārāca paṅktiḥ siṃhasya prāptā reje avidūrataḥ nīlotpala palāśānām mālā iva ujjvala darśanā tat sainyam utsāritavān tṛṇa agrāṇi iva mārutaḥ tatas aśma varṣam daitya indrāḥ vyamṛjanta nabhaḥ gatāḥ naga mātraiḥ śilā khaṇḍaiḥ giri śṛṅgaiḥ mahā prabhaiḥ diśaḥ daśa vikīrṇāḥ vai khadyota prakarāḥ iva tadā aśma oghaiḥ daitya gaṇāḥ punar siṃham ariṃdamam chādayāṃcakrire meghāḥ dhārābhiḥ iva parvatam dhārābhiḥ akṣa mātrābhiḥ prādurāsīt samantataḥ nabhasaḥ pracyutāḥ dhārāḥ tigma vegāḥ samantataḥ āvṛtya sarvatas vyoma diśaḥ ca tathā dhārāḥ divi ca sarvatra vasudhāyām ca sarvaśas na spṛśanti ca tāḥ devam nipatantyaḥ aniśam bhuvi bāhyatas vavṛṣuḥ varṣam na upariṣṭāt ca vavṛṣuḥ saḥ asṛjat dānavaḥ māyām agni vāyu samīritām mahā indraḥ toyadaiḥ sārdham sahasrākṣaḥ mahā dyutiḥ mahatā toya varṣeṇa śamayāmāsa pāvakam tasyām pratihatāyām tu māyāyām yudhi dānavaḥ asṛjat ghora saṃkāśam tamaḥ tīvram samantataḥ tamasā saṃvṛte loke daityeṣu ātta āyudheṣu ca tri śikhām bhrukuṭīm ca asya dadṛśuḥ dānavāḥ raṇe lalāṭa sthām triśūla aṅkām gaṅgām tripathagām iva tatas sarvāsu māyāsu hatāsu diti nandanāḥ hiraṇyakaśipum daityam vivarṇāḥ śaraṇam yayuḥ parāvahaḥ saṃvahaḥ ca mahā bala parākramāḥ tathā parivahaḥ śrīmān utpāta bhaya śaṃsinaḥ iti evam kṣubhitāḥ sapta marutaḥ gaganecarāḥ ayogataḥ ca api acarat mārgam niśi niśācaraḥ sagrahaḥ saha nakṣatraiḥ ākāpatiḥ ariṃdamaḥ vivarṇa tām ca bhagavān gataḥ divi divākaraḥ kṛṣṇam kabandham ca tathā lakṣyate su mahat divi amuñcat ca arciṣām vṛndam bhūmi vṛttiḥ vibhāvasuḥ gagana sthaḥ ca bhagavān abhīkṣṇam paridṛśyate sapta dhūmra nibhāḥ ghorāḥ sūryāḥ divi samutthitāḥ somasya gagana sthasya grahāḥ tiṣṭhanti śṛṅga gāḥ vāme tu dakṣiṇe ca eva sthitau śukra bṛhaspatī śanaiścaraḥ lohitāṅgaḥ jvalana aṅga sama dyutiḥ śṛṅgāṇi śanakais ghorāḥ yuga anta āvartinaḥ grahāḥ carācara vināśāya rohiṇīm na abhyanandata gṛhītaḥ rāhuṇā candraḥ ulkābhiḥ abhihanyate devānām api yaḥ devaḥ saḥ api avarṣata śoṇitam apatan gaganāt ulkāḥ vidyut rūpāḥ mahā svanāḥ latāḥ ca saphalāḥ sarvāḥ ye ca āhuḥ daityanāśanam phalaiḥ phalāni ajāyanta puṣpaiḥ puṣpam tathā eva ca āraṇyaiḥ saha saṃsṛṣṭāḥ grāmyāḥ ca mṛga pakṣiṇaḥ nadyaḥ ca pratikūlāni vahanti kaluṣa udakāḥ na prakāśanti ca diśaḥ rakta reṇu samākulāḥ vānaspatyaḥ na pūjyante pūjana arhāḥ kathaṃcana yadā ca sarva bhūtānām chāyā na parivartate aparāhṇa gate sūrye lokānām yuga saṃkṣaye bhāṇḍa āgāra āyudha āgāre niviṣṭam abhavat madhu asurāṇām vināśāya surāṇām vijayāya ca dṛśyante vividha utpātāḥ ghorāḥ ghora nidarśanāḥ ete ca anye ca bahavaḥ ghora utpātāḥ samutthitāḥ daitya indrasya vināśāya dṛśyante kāla nirmitāḥ medinyām kampamānāyām daitya indreṇa mahātmanā mahīdharāḥ nāga gaṇāḥ nipetuḥ amita ojasaḥ catur śīrṣāḥ pañca śīrṣāḥ sapta śīrṣāḥ ca pannagāḥ elāmukhaḥ kāliyaḥ ca mahāpadmaḥ ca vīryavān sahasra śīrṣā nāgaḥ vai hema tāla dhvajaḥ prabhuḥ śeṣaḥ anantaḥ mahābhāgaḥ duṣprakampyaḥ prakampitaḥ dīptāni antar jala sthāni pṛthivīdharaṇāni ca nāgāḥ tejaḥ dharāḥ ca api pātāla tala cāriṇaḥ hiraṇyakaśipuḥ daityaḥ tadā saṃspṛṣṭavān mahīm saṃdaṣṭa oṣṭha puṭaḥ krodhāt vārāhaḥ iva pūrvajaḥ nadī bhāgīrathī ca eva sarayūḥ kauśikī tathā yamunā tu atha kāverī kṛṣṇaveṇā ca nimnagā suveṇā ca mahābhāgā nadī godāvarī tathā carmaṇvatī ca sindhuḥ ca tathā nada nadīpatiḥ narmadā śubha toyā ca tathā vetravatī nadī gomatī go kula ākīrṇā tathā pūrva sarasvatī mahī kālamahī ca eva tamasā puṣpavāhinī suvarṇa prakaṭam ca eva suvarṇa ākara maṇḍitam mahānadam ca lauhityam śaila kānana śobhitam pattanam kośa karaṇam ṛṣi vīra jana ākaram māgadhāḥ ca mahāgrāmāḥ muṇḍāḥ śuṅgāḥ tathā eva ca suhmāḥ mallāḥ videhāḥ ca mālavāḥ kāśi kosalāḥ kailāsa śikhara ākāram yat kṛtam viśvakarmaṇā suvarṇa vedikaḥ śrīmān megha paṅkti niṣevitaḥ bhrājamānaḥ arka sadṛśaiḥ jātarūpa mayaiḥ drumaiḥ śālaiḥ tālaiḥ tamālaiḥ ca karṇikāraiḥ ca puṣpitaiḥ ayomukhaḥ ca vikhyātaḥ parvataḥ dhātu maṇḍitaḥ surāṣṭrāḥ ca sa bāhlīkāḥ śūra ābhīrāḥ tathā eva ca bhojāḥ pāṇḍyāḥ ca vaṅgāḥ ca kaliṅgāḥ tāmraliptakāḥ tathā eva auṇḍrāḥ ca pauṇḍrāḥ ca vāmacūḍāḥ sa keralāḥ kṣobhitāḥ tena daityena sa devāḥ ca apsaraḥ gaṇāḥ siddha cāraṇa saṃghaḥ ca viprakīrṇam manoharam jātarūpa mayaiḥ śṛṅgaiḥ apsaraḥ gaṇa nāditam giri puṣpitakaḥ ca eva lakṣmīvān priya darśanaḥ utthitaḥ sāgaram bhittvā viśrāmaḥ candra sūryayoḥ rarāja su mahā śṛṅgaiḥ gaganam vilikhan iva candra sūrya aṃśu saṃkāśaiḥ sāgara ambu samāvṛtaiḥ vidyutvān sarvatas śrīmān āyataḥ śata yojanam vidyutām yatra saṃpātāḥ nipātyante naga uttame ṛṣabhaḥ parvataḥ ca eva śrīmān vṛṣabha saṃjñitaḥ kuñjaraḥ parvataḥ śrīmān yatra agastya gṛham śubham viśālākṣaḥ ca durdharṣaḥ sarpāṇām ālayaḥ purī tathā bhogavatī ca api daitya indreṇa abhikampitā mahāsenaḥ giriḥ ca eva pāriyātraḥ ca parvataḥ cakravān ca giri śreṣṭhaḥ vārāhaḥ ca eva parvataḥ prāgjyautiṣa puram ca api jātarūpa mayam śubham yasmin vasati duṣṭa ātmā narakaḥ nāma dānavaḥ meghaḥ ca parvata śreṣṭhaḥ megha gambhīra niḥsvanaḥ ṣaṣṭiḥ tatra sahasrāṇi parvatānām dvijottamāḥ taruṇa āditya saṃkāśaḥ meruḥ tatra mahā giriḥ kampitam mānasam ca eva haṃsa kāraṇḍava ākulam triśṛṅga parvataḥ ca eva kumārī ca sarit varā tuṣāra caya saṃchannaḥ mandaraḥ ca api parvataḥ uśīrabinduḥ ca giriḥ candraprasthaḥ tathā adrirāj krauñcaḥ saptarṣi śailaḥ ca dhūmravarṇaḥ ca parvataḥ nadyaḥ sa sāgarāḥ sarvāḥ saḥ akampayata dānavaḥ kapilaḥ ca mahīputraḥ vyāghravān ca eva kampitaḥ khecarāḥ ca satī putrāḥ pātāla tala vāsinaḥ gaṇaḥ tathā paraḥ raudraḥ megha nāma aṅkuśa āyudhaḥ ūrdhva gaḥ bhīma vegaḥ ca sarvaḥ eva abhikampitāḥ gadī śūlī karālaḥ ca hiraṇyakaśipuḥ tadā jīmūta ghana saṃkāśaḥ jīmūta ghana niḥsvanaḥ jīmūta ghana nirghoṣaḥ jīmūtaḥ iva vegavān deva ariḥ ditijaḥ vīraḥ nṛsiṃham samupādravat samutpatya tatas tīkṣṇaiḥ mṛgendreṇa mahā nakhaiḥ mahī ca kālaḥ ca śaśī nabhaḥ ca grahāḥ ca sūryaḥ ca diśaḥ ca sarvāḥ nadyaḥ ca śailāḥ ca mahā arṇavāḥ ca gatāḥ prasādam diti putra nāśāt tatas pramuditāḥ devāḥ ṛṣayaḥ ca tapodhanāḥ tuṣṭuvuḥ nāmabhiḥ divyaiḥ ādidevam sanātanam yat tvayā vihitam deva nārasiṃham idam vapuḥ devāḥ ūcuḥ bhavān kartā vikartā ca lokānām prabhavaḥ avyayaḥ parām ca siddhim ca param ca devam param ca mantram paramam haviḥ ca param ca dharmam paramam ca viśvam tvām āhuḥ agryam puruṣam purāṇam param śarīram paramam ca brahma param ca yogam paramām ca vāṇīm param rahasyam paramām gatim ca tvām āhuḥ agryam puruṣam purāṇam param parasya api param ca bhūtam tvām āhuḥ agryam puruṣam purāṇam param parasya api param mahat yat tvām āhuḥ agryam puruṣam purāṇam param parasya api param ca dāntam tvām āhuḥ agryam puruṣam purāṇam stutvā nārāyaṇam devam brahma lokam gataḥ prabhuḥ tatas nadatsu tūryeṣu nṛtyantīṣu apsaraḥsu ca kṣīrodasya uttaram kūlam jagāma hariḥ īśvaraḥ nārasiṃham vapuḥ devaḥ sthāpayitvā su dīptimat paurāṇam rūpam āsthāya prayayau garuḍadhvajaḥ aṣṭa cakreṇa yānena bhūta yutena bhāsvatā Purāṇa ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ kathitam narasiṃhasya māhātmyam vistareṇa ca katham ca vaiṣṇavī sṛṣṭiḥ padma madhye abhavat purā sūtaḥ uvāca śrutvā ca narasiṃhasya māhātmyam ravinandanaḥ jala arṇava gatasya iha nābhau jātam janārdana puṣkare ca katham bhūtāḥ devāḥ sa ṛṣi gaṇāḥ purā etat ākhyāhi nikhilam yogam yoga vidām pate śṛṇvataḥ tasya me kīrtim na tṛptiḥ upajāyate kiyatā vā atha kālena hi uttiṣṭhati mahā yaśāḥ ke prajāpatayaḥ tāvat āsan pūrvam mahā mune katham nirmitavān ca eva citram lokam sanātanam katham ekārṇave śūnye naṣṭa sthāvara jaṅgame naṣṭa anila anale loke naṣṭa ākāśa mahī tale vibhuḥ mahābhūta patiḥ mahā tejāḥ mahā ākṛtiḥ āste sura vara śreṣṭhaḥ vidhim āsthāya yoga vid śṛṇuyām parayā bhaktyā brahman etat aśeṣataḥ vaktum arhasi dharmiṣṭha yaśaḥ nārāyaṇa ātmakam śraddhayā ca upaviṣṭānām bhagavan vaktum arhasi nārāyaṇasya yaśasaḥ śravaṇe yā tava spṛhā tat vaṃśya anvaya bhūtasya nyāyyam ravi kula ṛṣabha śṛṇuṣva ādi purāṇeṣu vedebhyaḥ ca yathā śrutam brāhmaṇānām ca vadatām śrutvā vai su mahātmanām yathā ca tapasā dṛṣṭvā bṛhaspati sama dyutiḥ parāśara sutaḥ śrīmān guruḥ dvaipāyanaḥ abravīt tat te aham kathayiṣyāmi yathāśakti yathāśruti yat vijñātum mayā śakyam ṛṣi mātreṇa sattamāḥ kaḥ samutsahate jñātum param nārāyaṇa ātmakam tat karma viśva vedānām tat rahasyam mahā ṛṣīṇām tam īśam sarva yajñānām tat tattvam sarva darśinām tat adhyātma vidām cintyam narakam ca vikarmiṇām tat bhūtam adhibhūtam ca tat param parama ṛṣīṇām yaḥ kartā kārakaḥ buddhiḥ manaḥ kṣetrajñaḥ eva ca praṇavaḥ puruṣaḥ śāstā ekaḥ ca iti vibhāvyate prāṇaḥ pañcavidhaḥ ca eva dhruvaḥ akṣaraḥ eva ca kālaḥ pākaḥ ca paktā ca draṣṭā svādhyāyaḥ eva ca saḥ asmān kārayate sarvān saḥ atyeti vyākulīkṛtān yajāmahe tam eva ādyam tam eva icchāma nirvṛtāḥ yāḥ kathāḥ ca eva vartante śrutayaḥ vā atha tad parāḥ viśvam viśvapatiḥ yaḥ ca sa tu nārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ Purāṇa catvāri āhuḥ sahasrāṇi varṣāṇām tu kṛtam yugam tasya tāvacchatī saṃdhyā dviguṇā ravinandana svadharma niratāḥ santaḥ jāyante yatra mānavāḥ viprāḥ sthitāḥ dharma parāḥ rāja vṛttau sthitāḥ nṛpāḥ kṛṣyām abhiratāḥ vaiśyāḥ śūdrāḥ śuśrūṣavaḥ sthitāḥ etat kārtayugam vṛttam sarveṣām api pārthiva prāṇinām dharma saṅgānām api vai nīca janmanām trīṇi varṣa sahasrāṇi tretā yugam iha ucyate tasya tāvacchatī saṃdhyā dviguṇā parikīrtyate dvābhyām adharmaḥ pādābhyām tribhiḥ dharmaḥ vyavasthitaḥ tretāyām vikṛtim yānti varṇāḥ tu ete na saṃśayaḥ cāturvarṇyasya vaikṛtyāt yānti daurbalyam āśramāḥ dvāparasya tu yā ceṣṭā tām api śrotum arhasi dvāparam dve sahasre tu varṣāṇām ravinandana tasya tāvacchatī saṃdhyā dviguṇā yugam ucyate tatra ca artha parāḥ sarve prāṇinaḥ rajasā hatāḥ sarve naiṣkṛtikāḥ kṣudrāḥ jāyante ravinandana tathā varṣa sahasram tu varṣāṇām dve śate api saṃdhyayā saha saṃkhyātam krūram kali yugam smṛtam yatra adharmaḥ catuṣpādaḥ syāt dharmaḥ pāda vigrahaḥ kāminaḥ tapasā hīnāḥ jāyante tatra mānavāḥ nāstikāḥ brahma bhaktāḥ vā jāyante tatra mānavāḥ ahaṃkāra gṛhītāḥ ca prakṣīṇa sneha bandhanāḥ viprāḥ śūdra samācārāḥ santi sarve kalau yuge āśramāṇām viparyāsaḥ kalau samparivartate varṇānām ca eva saṃdehaḥ yuga ante ravinandana vidyāt dvādaśasāhasrīm yuga ākhyām pūrva nirmitām śarīra nirvṛtim dṛṣṭvā loka saṃhāra buddhitaḥ devatānām ca sarvāsām brahma ādīnām mahīpate daityānām dānavānām ca yakṣa rākṣasa pakṣiṇām gandharvāṇām apsarasām bhujaṃgānām ca pārthiva parvatānām nadīnām ca paśūnām ca eva sattama tiryagyoni gatānām ca sattvānām kṛmiṇām tathā mahābhūta patiḥ pañca hṛtvā bhūtāni bhūta kṛt jagat saṃharaṇa arthāya kurute vaiśasam mahat bhūtvā sūryaḥ cakṣuṣī ca ādadānaḥ bhūtvā vāyuḥ prāṇinām prāṇa jālam Purāṇa bhūtvā nārāyaṇaḥ yogī sattva mūrtiḥ vibhāvasuḥ gabhastibhiḥ pradīptābhiḥ saṃśoṣayati sāgarān tatas pītvā arṇavān sarvān nadīḥ kūpān ca sarvaśas parvatānām ca salilam sarvam ādāya raśmibhiḥ bhittvā gabhastibhiḥ ca eva mahīm gatvā rasātalāt vāyuḥ ca balavān bhūtvā vidhunvānaḥ akhilam jagat prāṇa apāna samāna ādyān vāyūn ākarṣate hariḥ tatas deva gaṇāḥ sarve bhūtāni eva ca yāni tu gandhaḥ ghrāṇam śarīram ca pṛthivīm saṃśritāḥ guṇāḥ jihvā rasaḥ ca snehaḥ ca saṃśritāḥ salile guṇāḥ rūpam cakṣuḥ vipākaḥ ca jyotiḥ eva āśritāḥ guṇāḥ sparśaḥ prāṇaḥ ca ceṣṭā ca pavane saṃśritāḥ guṇāḥ śabdaḥ śrotram ca khāni eva gagane saṃśritāḥ guṇāḥ loka māyā bhagavatā muhūrtena vināśitā manaḥ buddhiḥ ca sarveṣām kṣetrajñaḥ ca iti yaḥ śrutaḥ tam vareṇyam parameṣṭhī hṛṣīkeśam upāśritaḥ vāyunā ākramyamāṇāsu druma śākhāsu ca āśritaḥ teṣām saṃgharṣaṇa udbhūtaḥ pāvakaḥ śatadhā jvalan adahat ca tadā sarvam vṛtaḥ saṃvartakaḥ analaḥ sa parvata drumān gulmān latā vallīḥ tṛṇāni ca vimānāni ca divyāni purāṇi vividhāni ca yāni ca āśrayaṇīyāni tāni sarvāṇi saḥ adahat bhasmīkṛtya tatas sarvān lokān lokaguruḥ hariḥ bhūyas nirvāpayāmāsa yuga antena ca karmaṇā sahasra vṛṣṭiḥ śatadhā bhūtvā kṛṣṇaḥ mahā balaḥ tatas kṣīra nikāyena svādunā parama ambhasā tena rodhena saṃchannā payasām varṣataḥ dharā sarva sattva vivarjitā naṣṭa arka pavana ākāśe sūkṣme jagati saṃvṛte saṃśoṣam ātmanā kṛtvā samudrān api dehinaḥ dagdhvā saṃplāvya ca tathā svapiti ekaḥ sanātanaḥ paurāṇam rūpam āsthāya svapiti amita vikramaḥ eka arṇava jala vyāpī yogī yogam upāśritaḥ anekāni sahasrāṇi yugāni eka arṇava ambhasi kaḥ ca eva puruṣaḥ nāma kiṃyogaḥ kaḥ ca yogavān asau kiyantam kālam ca ekārṇava vidhim prabhuḥ na draṣṭā na eva gamitā na jñātā na eva pārśvagaḥ nabhaḥ kṣitim pavanam apaḥ prakāśakam prajāpatim bhuvana dharam sura īśvaram pitāmaham śruti nilayam mahā munim praśāmya bhūyas śayanam hi arocayat Purāṇa evam ekārṇavībhūte śete loke mahā dyutiḥ pracchādya salilena urvīm haṃsaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ tadā virajaskam mahā bāhum akṣayam brahma yat viduḥ ātma rūpa prakāśena tamasā saṃvṛtaḥ prabhuḥ yāthātathyam param jñānam bhūtam tat brahmaṇā purā rahasi āraṇyaka uddiṣṭam yat ca aupaniṣadam smṛtam yaḥ ca anyaḥ puruṣa ākhyaḥ syāt saḥ eṣa puruṣottamaḥ ye ca yajña karāḥ viprāḥ ye ca ṛtvijaḥ iti smṛtāḥ asmāt eva purā bhūtāḥ yajñebhyaḥ śrūyatām tathā brahmāṇam prathamam vaktrāt udgātāram ca sāmagam hotāram api ca adhvaryum bāhubhyām asṛjat prabhuḥ brahmaṇaḥ brāhmaṇācchaṃsi prastotāram ca sarvaśas tau mitrāvaruṇau pṛṣṭhāt pratiprastāram eva ca udarāt pratihartāram potāram ca eva pārthiva acchāvākam atha ūrubhyām neṣṭāram ca eva pārthiva pāṇibhyām atha ca āgnīdhram subrahmaṇyam ca jānutaḥ grāvastutam tu pādābhyām unnetāram ca yājuṣam evam eva eṣa bhagavān ṣoḍaśa eva jagatpatiḥ pravaktṝn sarva yajñānām ṛtvijaḥ asṛjat uttamān tat eṣa vai veda mayaḥ puruṣaḥ yajña saṃjñitaḥ vedāḥ ca etad mayāḥ sarve sa aṅga upaniṣada kriyāḥ svapiti ekārṇave ca eva yat āścaryam abhūt purā śrūyatām tat yathā viprāḥ mārkaṇḍeya kutūhalam gīrṇaḥ bhagavataḥ tasya kukṣau eva mahā muniḥ aṭan tīrtha prasaṅgena pṛthivīm tīrtha gocarām āśramāṇi ca puṇyāni devatāyatanāni ca deśān rāṣṭrāṇi citrāṇi purāṇi vividhāni ca japa homa paraḥ śāntaḥ tapaḥ ghoram samāsthitaḥ mārkaṇḍeyaḥ tatas tasya śanais vaktrāt viniḥsṛtaḥ sa niṣkrāman na ca ātmānam jānīte deva māyayā tasya utpannam bhayam tīvram saṃśayaḥ ca ātma jīvite deva darśana saṃhṛṣṭaḥ vismayam paramam gataḥ cintayan jala madhya sthaḥ mārkaṇḍeyaḥ viśaṅkitaḥ vyaktam anyatamaḥ bhāvaḥ teṣām saṃbhāvitaḥ mama na hi īdṛśam jagat kleśam ayuktam satyam arhati naṣṭa candra arka pavane naṣṭa parvata bhū tale śarvaryām jāgratam iva bhāsantam svena tejasā devam draṣṭum iha āyātaḥ kaḥ bhavān iti vismayāt sampraviṣṭaḥ punar kukṣim mārkaṇḍeyaḥ ati vismayaḥ sa tathā eva yathāpūrvam yaḥ dharām aṭate purā puṇya tīrtha jala upetām vividhāni āśramāṇi ca kratubhiḥ yajamānāḥ ca samāpta vara dakṣiṇān apaśyat deva kukṣi sthān yājakān śataśas dvijān sat vṛttam āsthitāḥ sarve varṇāḥ brāhmaṇa pūrvakāḥ catvāraḥ ca āśramāḥ samyak yathā uddiṣṭāḥ mayā tava evam varṣa śatam sāgram mārkaṇḍeyasya dhīmataḥ carataḥ pṛthivīm sarvām na kukṣi antaḥ samīkṣitaḥ suptam nyagrodha śākhāyām bālam ekam niraikṣata tathā eva ekārṇava jale nīhāreṇa āvṛta ambare sa muniḥ vismaya āviṣṭaḥ kautūhala samanvitaḥ bālam āditya saṃkāśam na aśaknot abhivīkṣitum pūrva dṛṣṭam idam mene śaṅkitaḥ deva māyayā agādha salile tasmin mārkaṇḍeyaḥ sa vismayaḥ sa tasmai bhagavān āha svāgatam bāla yogavān mārkaṇḍeyaḥ muniḥ tu āha bālam tam śrama pīḍitaḥ mārkaṇḍeyaḥ uvāca kaḥ mām nāmnā kīrtayati tapaḥ paribhavan mama na hi eṣa vaḥ samācāraḥ deveṣu api mama ucitaḥ mām brahmā api hi deveśaḥ dīrgha āyuḥ iti bhāṣate mārkaṇḍeya iti mām uktvā mṛtyum īkṣitum arhati sūtaḥ uvāca evam ābhāṣya tam krodhāt mārkaṇḍeyaḥ mahā muniḥ tathā eva bhagavān bhūyas babhāṣe madhusūdanaḥ śrī bhagavān uvāca aham te janakaḥ vatsa hṛṣīkeśaḥ pitā guruḥ āyuṣpradātā paurāṇaḥ kim mām tvam na upasarpasi mām putra kāmaḥ prathamam pitā te aṅgirasaḥ muniḥ pūrvam ārādhayāmāsa tapaḥ tīvram samāśritaḥ tatas tvām ghora tapasā prāvṛṇot amita ojasam kaḥ samutsahate ca anyaḥ yaḥ na bhūta ātmaka ātmajaḥ draṣṭum ekārṇava gatam krīḍantam yoga vartmanā mūrdhni baddha añjali puṭaḥ mārkaṇḍeyaḥ mahā tapāḥ nāma gotre tatas procya dīrgha āyuḥ loka pūjitaḥ mārkaṇḍeyaḥ uvāca iccheyam tattvataḥ māyām imām jñātum tava anagha yat ekārṇava madhya sthaḥ śeṣe tvam bāla rūpavān kiṃsaṃjñaḥ ca eva bhagavān loke vijñāyase prabho tarkaye tvām mahātmānam kaḥ hi anyaḥ sthātum arhati śrī bhagavān uvāca aham nārāyaṇaḥ brahman sarva bhūta vināśanaḥ aham sahasra śīrṣa ākhyaḥ yaḥ padaiḥ abhisaṃjñitaḥ aham indra pade śakraḥ varṣāṇām parivatsaraḥ aham sarvāṇi sattvāni daivatāni akhilāni tu bhujaṃgānām aham śeṣaḥ tārkṣyaḥ vai sarva pakṣiṇām kṛtāntaḥ sarva bhūtānām viśveṣām kāla saṃjñitaḥ aham dharmaḥ tapaḥ ca aham sarva āśrama nivāsinām aham ca eva sarit divyā kṣīrodaḥ ca mahā arṇavaḥ aham ijyā kriyā ca aham aham vidyādhipaḥ smṛtaḥ aham jyotiḥ aham vāyuḥ aham bhūmiḥ aham nabhaḥ aham āpaḥ samudrāḥ ca nakṣatrāṇi diśaḥ daśa aham varṣam aham somaḥ parjanyaḥ aham aham raviḥ kṣīroda sāgare ca aham samudre vaḍavāmukhaḥ vahniḥ saṃvartakaḥ bhūtvā piban toya mayam haviḥ aham purāṇaḥ paramam tathā eva aham parāyaṇam yat kiṃcid paśyase vipra yat śṛṇoṣi ca kiṃcana viśvam sṛṣṭam mayā pūrvam sṛjyam ca adya api paśya mām yuge yuge ca srakṣyāmi mārkaṇḍeya akhilam jagat tat etat akhilam sarvam mārkaṇḍeya avadhāraya śuśrūṣuḥ mama dharmān ca kukṣau cara sukham mama mama brahmā śarīra sthaḥ devaiḥ ca ṛṣibhiḥ saha evamādi purāṇa īśaḥ vadan eva mahāmatiḥ vaktram āhṛtavān āśu mārkaṇḍeyam mahā munim tatas bhagavataḥ kukṣim praviṣṭaḥ muni sattamaḥ Purāṇa āpavaḥ sa vibhuḥ bhūtvā cārayāmāsa vai tapaḥ chādayitvā ātmanaḥ deham yādasām kula saṃbhavam mahatām pañcabhūtānām viśvaḥ viśvam acintayat īṣat saṃkṣobhayāmāsa saḥ arṇavam salila āśrayaḥ śabdam prati tadā udbhūtaḥ mārutaḥ chidra saṃbhavaḥ vivardhatā balavatā vegāt vikṣobhitaḥ arṇavaḥ kṛṣṇavartmā samabhavat prabhuḥ vaiśvānaraḥ mahān tatas saṃśoṣayāmāsa pāvakaḥ salilam bahu ātma teja udbhavāḥ puṇyāḥ āpaḥ amṛta rasa upamāḥ ākāśam chidra sambhūtam vāyuḥ ākāśa saṃbhavaḥ ābhyām saṃgharṣaṇa udbhūtam pāvakam vāyu saṃbhavam dṛṣṭvā prītaḥ mahādevaḥ mahā bhūta vibhāvanaḥ dṛṣṭvā bhūtāni bhagavān loka sṛṣṭi artham uttamam yat pṛthivyām dvijendrāṇām tapasā bhāvitātmanām jñānam vṛṣṭam tu viśva arthe yoginām yāti mukhya tām tam yogavantam vijñāya sampūrṇa aiśvaryam uttamam padmam nābhi udbhavam ca ekam samutpāditavān tadā sahasra parṇam virajam bhāskara ābham hiraṇmayam Purāṇa atha yogavatām śreṣṭham asṛjat bhūri tejasam sraṣṭāram sarva lokānām brahmāṇam sarvatomukham tat ca padmam purāṇa jñāḥ pṛthivī rūpam uttamam nārāyaṇa samudbhūtam pravadanti mahā ṛṣayaḥ yā padmā sā rasā devī pṛthivī paricakṣyate kailāsam muñjavantam ca tathā anyam gandhamādanam ete deva gaṇānām ca siddhānām ca mahātmanām āśrayāḥ puṇya śīlānām sarva kāma phala pradāḥ eteṣām antare deśaḥ jambūdvīpaḥ iti smṛtaḥ jambūdvīpasya saṃsthānam yajñiyāḥ yatra vai kriyāḥ divyāḥ tīrtha śata ādhārāḥ su ramyāḥ saritaḥ smṛtāḥ smṛtāni yāni padmasya kesarāṇi samantataḥ asaṃkhyeyāḥ pṛthivyāḥ te viśve vai dhātu parvatāḥ yāni padmasya parṇāni bhūrīṇi tu narādhipa te durgamāḥ śaila citāḥ mleccha deśāḥ vikalpitāḥ yāni adhobhāga parṇāni te nivāsāḥ tu bhāgaśas daityānām uragāṇām ca pataṅgānām ca pārthiva teṣām mahā arṇavaḥ yatra tat rasa iti abhisaṃjñitam mahāpātaka karmāṇaḥ majjante yatra mānavāḥ proktā atha dikṣu sarvāsu catvāraḥ salila ākarāḥ evam nārāyaṇasya arthe mahī puṣkara saṃbhavā prādurbhāvaḥ api ayam tasmāt nāmnā puṣkara saṃjñitaḥ etasmāt kāraṇāt tad jñaiḥ purāṇaiḥ parama ṛṣibhiḥ yājñikaiḥ veda dṛṣṭāntaiḥ yajñe padma vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ evam bhagavatā tena viśveṣām dhāraṇā vidhiḥ parvatānām nadīnām ca hradānām ca eva nirmitaḥ śanais svayaṃbhūḥ śayanam sṛjat tadā jagat mayam padma vidhim mahā arṇave Purāṇa divya rakta ambara dharau śveta dīpta ugra daṃṣṭriṇau mahā vivṛta tāmra akṣau pīna uraskau mahā bhujau nava megha pratīkāśau āditya sadṛśa ānanau vidyut ābhau gadā agrābhyām karābhyām ati bhīṣaṇau tau pādayoḥ tu vinyāsāt utkṣipantau iva arṇavam kampayantau iva harim śayānam madhusūdanam yoginām śreṣṭham āsādya dīptam dadṛśatuḥ tadā nārāyaṇa samājñātam sṛjantam akhilāḥ prajāḥ daivatāni ca viśvāni mānasān asurān ṛṣīn dīptau mumūrṣū saṃkruddhau roṣa vyākulita īkṣaṇau kaḥ tvam puṣkara madhya sthaḥ sita uṣṇīṣaḥ catur bhujaḥ ādhāya niyamam mohāt āste tvam vigata jvaraḥ āvābhyām param īśābhyām aśaktaḥ tvam iha arṇave kaḥ sraṣṭā kaḥ ca te goptā kena nāmnā vidhīyase madhu kaiṭabhau ūcatuḥ āvābhyām chādyate viśvam tamasā rajasā atha vai rajaḥ tamaḥ mayau āvām ṛṣīṇām avalaṅghitau āvābhyām uhyate lokaḥ duṣkarābhyām yuge yuge āvām arthaḥ ca kāmaḥ ca yajñaḥ svarga parigrahaḥ sukham yatra mudā yuktam yatra śrīḥ kīrtiḥ eva ca yeṣām yat kāṅkṣitam ca eva tat tat āvām vicintaya yatnāt yogavataḥ dṛṣṭyā yogaḥ pūrvam mayā arjitaḥ tam samādhāya guṇavat sattvam ca asmi samāśritaḥ rajasaḥ tamasaḥ ca eva yaḥ sraṣṭā viśva saṃbhavaḥ tatas bhūtāni jāyante sāttvikāni itarāṇi ca saḥ eva hi yuvām na aṃśe vaśī devaḥ haniṣyati bāhum nārāyaṇaḥ brahmā kṛtavān ātma māyayā kṛṣyamāṇau tatas tasya bāhunā bāhuśālinaḥ tatas tau āhatuḥ gatvā tadā devam sanātanam padmanābham hṛṣīkeśam praṇipatya sthitau ubhau jānīvaḥ tvām viśva yonim tvām ekam puruṣottamam tvam āvām pāhi hetu artham idam nau buddhi kāraṇam amogha darśanaḥ sa tvam yatas tvām vidvaḥ śāśvatam tatas tvām āgatau āvām abhitas prasamīkṣitum śrī bhagavān uvāca madhu kaiṭabhau ūcatuḥ śrī bhagavān uvāca bāḍham yuvām tu pravarau bhaviṣyat kāla saṃbhave varam pradāya atha mahā asurābhyām sanātanaḥ viśva varaḥ surottamaḥ Purāṇa ūrdhvabāhuḥ mahā tejāḥ tapaḥ ghoram samāśritaḥ babhāse sarva dharma sthaḥ sahasrāṃśuḥ iva aṃśubhiḥ atha anyat rūpam āsthāya śaṃbhuḥ nārāyaṇaḥ avyayaḥ ājagāma mahā tejāḥ yoga ācāryaḥ mahā yaśāḥ sāṃkhya ācāryaḥ hi matimān kapilaḥ brāhmaṇaḥ varaḥ parāvara viśeṣa jñau pūjitau ca mahā ṛṣibhiḥ brahma ātma dṛḍha bandhaḥ ca viśālaḥ jagat āsthitaḥ grāmaṇīḥ sarva bhūtānām brahmā trailokya pūjitaḥ trīn imān kṛtavān lokān yathā iyam brahmaṇaḥ śrutiḥ putram ca śaṃbhave ca ekam samutpāditavān ṛṣiḥ saḥ utpanna mātraḥ brahmāṇam uktavān mānasaḥ sutaḥ kim kurmaḥ tava sāhāyyam bravītu bhagavān ṛṣiḥ śuśrūṣuḥ asmi yuvayoḥ kim karomi kṛtāñjaliḥ śrī bhagavān uvāca yat satyam akṣaram brahma hi aṣṭādaśavidham tu tat saṃkalpayitvā manasā tam eva ca mahāmanāḥ tatas saḥ atha abravīt vākyam kim karomi pitāmaha pitāmaha samājñātaḥ brahmāṇam samupasthitaḥ brahma abhyāsam tu kṛtavān bhuvaḥ ca pṛthivīm gataḥ prāptaḥ ca paramam sthānam sa tayoḥ pārśvam āgataḥ mokṣa pravṛtti kuśalam bhūrbhuvam nāmataḥ vibhum evam putrāḥ trayaḥ api ete uktāḥ śaṃbhoḥ mahātmanaḥ tān gṛhītvā sutān tasya prayātaḥ sva arjitām gatim nārāyaṇaḥ ca bhagavān kapilaḥ ca yati īśvaraḥ tatas ghoratamam bhūyas saṃśritaḥ paramam vratam śarīra ardhāt tatas bhāryām samutpāditavān śubhām sadṛśīm ātmanaḥ devīm samarthām loka sarjane tatas jagāda tripadām gāyatrīm veda pūjitām sṛjan prajānām patayaḥ sāgarān ca asṛjat vibhuḥ aparān ca eva caturaḥ vedān gāyatri saṃbhavān ātmanaḥ sadṛśān putrān asṛjat vai pitāmahaḥ viśve prajānām patayaḥ yebhyaḥ lokāḥ viniḥsṛtāḥ viśveśam prathamam tāvat mahā tāpasam ātmajam sarva mantra hitam puṇyam nāmnā dharmam sa sṛṣṭavān vasiṣṭham gautamam ca eva bhṛgum aṅgirasam manum atha eva adbhutam iti ete jñeyāḥ paitāmaha ṛṣayaḥ trayodaśa guṇam dharmam ālabhanta mahā ṛṣayaḥ aditiḥ ditiḥ danuḥ kālā anāyuḥ siṃhikā muniḥ dakṣasya apatyam etāḥ vai kanyāḥ dvādaśa pārthiva marīceḥ kaśyapaḥ putraḥ tapasā nirmitaḥ kila tasmai kanyāḥ dvādaśa anyāḥ dakṣaḥ tāḥ pradadau tadā nakṣatrāṇi ca somāya tadā vai dattavān ṛṣiḥ rohiṇī ādīni sarvāṇi puṇyāni ravinandana lakṣmīḥ marutvatī sādhyā viśveśā ca matā śubhā devī sarasvatī ca eva brahmaṇā nirmitāḥ purā etāḥ pañca variṣṭhāḥ vai suraśreṣṭhāya pārthiva dattā bhadrāya dharmāya brahmaṇā dṛṣṭa karmaṇā yā tu rūpavatī patnī brahmaṇaḥ kāmarūpiṇī surabhiḥ sā hitā bhūtvā brahmāṇam samupasthitā jajñire ca sutāḥ tasyām vipulāḥ dhūma saṃnibhāḥ nakta saṃdhyā abhra saṃkāśāḥ prādahan tigma tejasaḥ rodanāt ravaṇāt ca eva rudrāḥ iti tatas smṛtāḥ nirṛtiḥ ca eva śaṃbhuḥ vai tṛtīyaḥ ca aparājitaḥ mṛgavyādhaḥ kapardī ca dahanaḥ atha īśvaraḥ ca vai ahirbudhnyaḥ ca bhagavān kapālī ca api piṅgalaḥ senānīḥ ca mahātejāḥ rudrāḥ tu ekādaśa smṛtāḥ tasyām eva surabhyām ca gāvaḥ yajña īśvarāḥ ca vai prakṛṣṭāḥ ca tathā māyāḥ surabhyāḥ paśavaḥ akṣarāḥ ajāḥ ca eva tu haṃsāḥ ca tathā eva amṛtam uttamam oṣadhyaḥ pravarāyāḥ ca samutthitāḥ dharmāt lakṣmīḥ tathā kāmam sādhyā sādhyān vyajāyata bhavam ca prabhavam ca eva hi īśam ca asuḥ aham tathā aruṇam ca aruṇim ca eva viśvāvasu bala dhruvau haviṣyam ca vitānam ca vidhāna śamitau api vatsaram ca eva bhūtim ca sarva asura niṣūdanam suparvāṇam bṛhat kāntiḥ sādhyāḥ loka namaskṛtāḥ vāsava anugatā devī janayāmāsa vai surān viśvāvasum tṛtīyam ca caturtham somam īśvaram saptamam ca tathā vāyum aṣṭamam nirṛtim vasum viśve devāḥ ca viśvāyām dharmāt jātāḥ iti śrutiḥ dakṣaḥ ca eva mahābāhuḥ puṣkara svanaḥ eva ca viśrāntaka vapurbālaḥ viṣkambhaḥ ca mahā yaśāḥ viśvān devān deva mātā viśveśā janayat sutān amaram śaravṛṣṭim ca sukarṣam ca mahā bhujam virājam ca eva vācam ca viśvāvasu matim tathā aśvamitram citraraśmim tathā niṣadhanam nṛpa hūyantam vāḍavam ca eva cāritram mandapannagam bṛhantam vai bṛhadrūpam tathā vai pūtanā anugam marutvatī purā jajñe etān vai marutām gaṇān aditiḥ kaśyapāt jajñe ādityān dvādaśa eva hi indraḥ viṣṇuḥ bhagaḥ tvaṣṭā varuṇaḥ hi aryamā raviḥ pūṣā mitraḥ ca dhanadaḥ dhātā parjanyaḥ eva ca iti ete dvādaśa ādityāḥ variṣṭhāḥ tridivaukasaḥ tapaḥ śreṣṭhau guṇa śreṣṭhau tridivasya api saṃmatau danuḥ tu dānavān jajñe ditiḥ daityān vyajāyata kālā tu vai kālakeyān asurān surasā tu vai anāyuṣāyāḥ tanayāḥ vyādhayaḥ su mahā balāḥ siṃhikā graha mātā vai gandharva jananī muniḥ tāmrā tu apsarasām mātā puṇyānām bhārata udbhava krodhāyāḥ sarva bhūtāni piśācāḥ ca eva pārthiva jajñe yakṣa gaṇān ca eva rākṣasān ca viśām pate catuṣpadāni sattvāni tathā gāvaḥ tu saurabhāḥ suparṇān pakṣiṇaḥ ca eva vinatā ca vyajāyata mahīdharān sarva nāgān devī kadrūḥ vyajāyata evam vṛddhim samagaman viśve lokāḥ paraṃtapa tadā vai pauṣkaraḥ rājan prādurbhāvaḥ mahātmanaḥ prādurbhāvaḥ pauṣkaraḥ te mayā dvaipāyana īritaḥ purāṇaḥ puruṣaḥ ca eva mayā viṣṇuḥ hariḥ prabhuḥ yaḥ ca idam agryam śṛṇuyāt purāṇam sadā naraḥ parvasu gauraveṇa cakṣuṣā manasā vācā karmaṇā ca caturvidham prasādayati yaḥ kṛṣṇam tam kṛṣṇaḥ anuprasīdati kṣīṇa āyuḥ labhate ca āyuḥ putra kāmaḥ sutam tathā yajñāḥ vedāḥ tathā kāmāḥ tapāṃsi vividhāni ca prāpnoti vividham puṇyam viṣṇu bhaktaḥ dhanāni ca sarvam vihāya yaḥ imam paṭhet pauṣkarakam hareḥ prādurbhāvam nṛpa śreṣṭha na tasya hi aśubham bhavet eṣa pauṣkarakaḥ nāma prādurbhāvaḥ mahātmanaḥ kīrtitaḥ te mahābhāga vyāsa śruti nidarśanāt Purāṇa viṣṇu tvam śṛṇu viṣṇoḥ ca hari tvam ca kṛte yuge vaikuṇṭha tvam ca deveṣu kṛṣṇa tvam mānuṣeṣu ca īśvarasya hi tasya eṣā karmaṇām gahanā gatiḥ saṃprati atītān bhavyān ca śṛṇu rājan yathātatham eṣa nārāyaṇaḥ bhūtvā hariḥ āsīt sanātanaḥ brahmā vāyuḥ ca somaḥ ca dharmaḥ śakraḥ bṛhaspatiḥ eṣa viṣṇuḥ iti khyātaḥ indrasya avarajaḥ vibhuḥ vadha artham sura śatrūṇām daitya dānava rakṣasām pradhāna ātmā purā hi eṣa brahmāṇam asṛjat prabhuḥ saḥ asṛjat pūrvapuruṣaḥ purākalpe prajāpatīn asṛjat mānavān tatra brahma vaṃśān anuttamān etat āścarya bhūtasya viṣṇoḥ karma anukīrtanam kīrtanīyasya lokeṣu kīrtyamānam nibodha me āsīt trailokya vikhyātaḥ saṅgrāmaḥ tārakā mayaḥ yatra te dānavāḥ ghorāḥ sarve saṅgrāma durjayāḥ ghnanti deva gaṇān sarvān sa yakṣa uraga rākṣasān te vadhyamānāḥ vimukhāḥ kṣīṇa praharaṇāḥ raṇe trātāram manasā jagmuḥ devam nārāyaṇam prabhum etasmin antare meghāḥ nirvāṇa aṅgāra varcasaḥ caṇḍāḥ vidyut gaṇa upetāḥ ghora nirhrāda kāriṇaḥ anyonya vega abhihatāḥ pravavuḥ saha mārutāḥ vajra vega anala anilaiḥ arūpavanti rūpāṇi tasmin utpāta lakṣaṇe jātam ca niṣprabham sarvam na prājñāyata kiṃcana viveśa rūpiṇī kālī kāla megha avaguṇṭhitā dyauḥ na bhāti abhibhūta arkā ghoreṇa tamasā āvṛtā tān ghana oghān sa timirān dorbhyām ākṣipya sa prabhuḥ vapuḥ saṃdarśayāmāsa divyam kṛṣṇa vapuḥ hariḥ balāhaka añjana nibham balāhaka tanūruham tejasā vapuṣā ca eva kṛṣṇam kṛṣṇam iva acalam dīpta pīta ambara dharam tapta kāñcana bhūṣaṇam dhūma andhakāra vapuṣam yugānta agnim iva utthitam catur dviguṇa pīna aṃsam kirīṭa channa mūrdhajam nandaka ānandita karam śara āśīviṣa dhāriṇam viṣṇu śailam kṣamā mūlam śrīvṛkṣam śārṅga śṛṅgiṇam vidyā ahaṃkāra sāra āḍhyam mahābhūta prarohaṇam sāgara ākāra nirhrādam rasātala mahā āśrayam mṛgendra pāśaiḥ vitatam pakṣa jantu niṣevitam śīla artha cāru gandha āḍhyam sarva loka mahā drumam vimāna vihaga vyātam toyada āḍambara ākulam jantu matsya gaṇa ākīrṇam śaila śaṅkha kulaiḥ yutam vīravṛkṣa latā gulmam bhujaga utkṛṣṭa śaivalam dvādaśa arka mahā dvīpam rudra ekādaśa pattanam daitya rakṣaḥ gaṇa grāham yakṣa uraga jhaṣa ākulam pitāmaha mahā vīryam sarva strī ratna śobhitam śrī kīrti kānti lakṣmībhiḥ nadībhiḥ upaśobhitam tam tu yoga mahā pāram nārāyaṇa mahā arṇavam anugraha karam devam praśānti karaṇam śubham te kṛtāñjalayaḥ sarve devāḥ śakra purogamāḥ jayaśabdam puraskṛtya śaraṇyam śaraṇam gatāḥ sa teṣām tām giram śrutvā viṣṇuḥ daivata daivatam manaḥ cakre vināśāya dānavānām mahā mṛdhe ākāśe tu sthitaḥ viṣṇuḥ uttamam vapuḥ āsthitaḥ uvāca devatāḥ sarvāḥ sapratijñam idam vacaḥ śāntim vrajata bhadram vaḥ mā bhaiṣṭa marutām gaṇāḥ jitāḥ me dānavāḥ sarve trailokyam parigṛhyatām te tasya satya saṃdhasya viṣṇoḥ vākyena toṣitāḥ devāḥ prītim samājagmuḥ prāśya amṛtam iva uttamam tatas tamaḥ saṃhṛtam tat vineśuḥ ca balāhakāḥ pravavuḥ ca śivāḥ vātāḥ praśāntāḥ ca diśaḥ daśa śuddha prabhāṇi jyotīṃṣi somaḥ cakruḥ pradakṣiṇām na vigraham grahāḥ cakruḥ praśāntāḥ ca api sindhavaḥ virajaskāḥ abhavan mārgāḥ nāka varga ādayaḥ trayaḥ yathārtham ūhuḥ saritaḥ na api cukṣubhire arṇavāḥ āsan śubhāni indriyāṇi narāṇām antarātmasu mahā ṛṣayaḥ vīta śokāḥ vedān uccais adhīyata yajñeṣu ca haviḥ pākam śivam āpa ca pāvakaḥ pravṛtta dharmāḥ saṃvṛttāḥ lokāḥ mudita mānasāḥ Purāṇa tatas abhayam viṣṇu vacaḥ śrutvā daityāḥ ca dānavāḥ kiṅkiṇī jāla nirghoṣam dvīpi carma pariṣkṛtam ruciram ratna jālaiḥ ca hema jālaiḥ ca śobhitam īhāmṛga gaṇa ākīrṇam pakṣi paṅkti virājitam divya astra tūṇīra dharam payodhara vināditam gadā parigha sampūrṇam mūrtimantam iva arṇavam hema keyūra valayam svarṇa maṇḍala kūbaram yuktam ṛkṣa sahasreṇa samṛddha ambuda nāditam dīptam ākāśa gam divyam ratham para ratha arujam adhyatiṣṭhat raṇa ākāṅkṣī merum dīptaḥ iva aṃśumān śaila ākāram asaṃbādham nīlāñjana caya upamam kārṣṇāyasa mayam divyam loha īṣā ābaddha kūbaram loha jālena mahatā sa gavākṣeṇa daṃśitam āyasaiḥ parighaiḥ pūrṇam kṣepaṇīyaiḥ ca mudgaraiḥ prāsaiḥ pāśaiḥ ca vitataiḥ a saṃyuktaiḥ ca kaṇṭakaiḥ śobhitam trāsa yānaiḥ ca tomaraiḥ ca paraśvadhaiḥ pramukhe tasya sainyasya dīpta śṛṅgaḥ iva acalaḥ syandanam vāhayāmāsa sapatna anīka mardanaḥ kharaḥ tu vikṣaran darpāt netrābhyām roṣa jam jalam sphurat danta uṣṭha nayanaḥ saṅgrāmam saḥ abhyakāṅkṣata tvaṣṭā tu aṣṭa gajam ghoram yānam āsthāya dānavaḥ vyūhitum dānava vyūham paricakrāma vīryavān vipracitti sutaḥ śvetaḥ śveta kuṇḍala bhūṣaṇaḥ śveta śaila pratīkāśaḥ yuddhāya abhimukhe sthitaḥ ariṣṭaḥ bali putraḥ ca variṣṭhaḥ adri śilā āyudhaḥ kiśoraḥ tu ati saṃharṣāt kiśoraḥ iti coditaḥ sa balāḥ dānavāḥ ca eva saṃnahyante yathākramam lambaḥ tu nava megha ābhaḥ pralamba ambara bhūṣaṇaḥ daitya vyūha gataḥ bhāti sa nīhāraḥ iva aṃśumān svarbhānuḥ āsya yodhī tu daśana uṣṭha īkṣaṇa āyudhaḥ hasan tiṣṭhati daityānām pramukhe sa mahā grahaḥ siṃha vyāghra gatāḥ ca anye varāha ṛkṣeṣu ca apare kecid khara uṣṭra yātāraḥ kecid śvāpada vāhanāḥ pattinaḥ tu apare daityāḥ bhīṣaṇāḥ vikṛta ānanāḥ eka pāda ardha pādāḥ ca nanṛtuḥ yuddha kāṅkṣiṇaḥ āsphoṭayantaḥ bahavaḥ kṣveḍantaḥ ca tathā pare hṛṣṭa śārdūla nirghoṣāḥ neduḥ dānava puṃgavāḥ te gadā parighaiḥ ugraiḥ śilā musala pāṇayaḥ pāśaiḥ prāsaiḥ ca parighaiḥ tomara aṅkuśa paṭṭiśaiḥ cikrīḍuḥ te śataghnībhiḥ śatadhāraiḥ ca mudgaraiḥ gaṇḍaśailaiḥ ca śailaiḥ ca parighaiḥ ca uttama āyasaiḥ Purāṇa surāṇām api sainyasya vistāram vaiṣṇavam śṛṇu sa balāḥ sa anugāḥ ca eva saṃnahyanta yathākramam grāmaṇīḥ sarva devānām āruroha sura dvipam dīptimadbhiḥ sadasyaiḥ ca brahmarṣibhiḥ abhiṣṭutaḥ yam ārūḍhaḥ sa bhagavān paryeti sakalam jagat svarge śakra anuyāteṣu deva tūrya ninādiṣu sundaryaḥ parinṛtyanti śataśas apsarasām gaṇāḥ ketunā nāga rājena rājamānaḥ yathā raviḥ tasthau sura gaṇa anīke daityān nādena bhīṣayan kālapāśān samāvidhyan hayaiḥ śaśi kara upamaiḥ vāyu īritaiḥ jala ākāraiḥ kurvan līlāḥ sahasraśas pāṇḍura uddhūta vasanaḥ pravāla rucira aṅgadaḥ maṇi śyāma uttama vapuḥ hari bhāra arpitaḥ varaḥ varuṇaḥ pāśa dhṛk madhye deva anīkasya tasthivān yakṣa rākṣasa sainyena guhyakānām gaṇaiḥ api yuktaḥ ca śaṅkha padmābhyām nidhīnām adhipaḥ prabhuḥ rājarāja īśvaraḥ śrīmān gadā pāṇiḥ adṛśyata sa rāja rājaḥ śuśubhe yuddha arthī naravāhanaḥ ukṣāṇam āsthitaḥ saṃkhye sākṣāt iva śivaḥ svayam pūrvapakṣaḥ sahasrākṣaḥ pitṛrājaḥ tu dakṣiṇaḥ varuṇaḥ paścimam pakṣam uttaram naravāhanaḥ caturṣu yuktāḥ catvāraḥ lokapālāḥ mahā balāḥ sūryaḥ sapta aśva yuktena rathena amita gāminā śriyā jājvalyamānena dīpyamānaiḥ ca raśmibhiḥ cacāra madhye lokānām dvādaśa ātmā dineśvaraḥ somaḥ śveta haye bhāti syandane śīta raśmivān himavat toya pūrṇābhiḥ bhābhiḥ āhlādayan jagat tam ṛkṣa pūga anugatam śiśirāṃśum dvijeśvaram śaśa chāyā aṅkita tanum naiśasya tamasaḥ kṣayam jyotiṣām īśvaram vyomni rasānām rasa dam prabhum oṣadhīnām sahasrāṇām nidhānam amṛtasya ca dadṛśuḥ dānavāḥ somam hima praharaṇam sthitam yaḥ prāṇaḥ sarva bhūtānām pañcadhā bhidyate nṛṣu sapta dhātu gataḥ lokān trīn dadhāra cacāra ca yam vadanti uttamam bhūtam yam vadanti aśarīriṇam yam āhuḥ ākāśa gamam śīghra gam śabda yoginam sa vāyuḥ sarva bhūta āyuḥ udbhūtaḥ svena tejasā śara bhūtāḥ divi indrāṇām ceruḥ vyātta ānanāḥ divi parvataiḥ ca śilā śṛṅgaiḥ śataśas ca eva pādapaiḥ upatasthuḥ sura gaṇāḥ prahartum dānavam balam yaḥ sa devaḥ hṛṣīkeśaḥ padmanābhaḥ trivikramaḥ yugānta kṛṣṇavartma ābhaḥ viśvasya jagataḥ prabhuḥ bhūmi āpaḥ vyoma bhūta ātmā śyāmaḥ śānti karaḥ ari hā ari ghnam amara ādīnām cakram gṛhya gadā dharaḥ savyena ālambya mahatīm sarva asura vināśinīm kareṇa kālīm vapuṣā śatru kāla pradām gadām dadhāra āyudha jātāni śārṅga ādīni mahā balaḥ pavana adhika saṃpātam gagana kṣobhaṇam khagam deva asura vimardeṣu bahuśas dṛṣṭa vikramam mahā indreṇa amṛtasya arthe vajreṇa kṛta lakṣaṇam śikhinam balinam ca eva tapta kuṇḍala bhūṣaṇam sphīta kroḍa avalambena śītāṃśu sama tejasā pakṣābhyām cāru patrābhyām āvṛtya divi līlayā yuga ante sa indracāpābhyām toyadābhyām iva ambaram nīla lohita pītābhiḥ patākābhiḥ alaṃkṛtam aruṇāvarajam śrīmān āruhya samare vibhuḥ suvarṇa varṇa vapuṣā suparṇam khecara uttamam tam anvayuḥ deva gaṇāḥ munayaḥ ca samāhitāḥ gīrbhiḥ parama mantrābhiḥ tuṣṭuvuḥ ca janārdanam tat vaiśravaṇa saṃśliṣṭam vaivasvata puraḥsaram pavana āviddha nirghoṣam saṃpradīpta hutāśanam viṣṇoḥ jiṣṇoḥ ca bhrājiṣṇoḥ tejasā tamasā āvṛtam svasti astu dānava anīke uśanāḥ vākyam ādade Purāṇa tābhyām balābhyām saṃjajñe tumulaḥ vigrahaḥ tadā surāṇām asurāṇām ca paraspara jaya eṣiṇām dānavāḥ daivataiḥ sārdham nānā praharaṇa udyatāḥ kṣipyamāṇaiḥ ca musalaiḥ saṃpatadbhiḥ ca sāyakaiḥ cāpaiḥ visphāryamāṇaiḥ ca pātyamānaiḥ ca mudgaraiḥ te vadhyamānāḥ balibhiḥ dānavaiḥ jitakāśibhiḥ viṣaṇṇa vadanāḥ devāḥ jagmuḥ ārtim parām mṛdhe veṣṭitāḥ śara jālaiḥ ca niryatnāḥ ca asuraiḥ kṛtāḥ praviṣṭāḥ dānavīm māyām na śekuḥ te viceṣṭitum astam gatam iva ābhāti niṣprāṇa sadṛśa ākṛti balam surāṇām asuraiḥ niṣprayatna āyudham kṛtam daitya cāpa cyutān ghorān chittvā vajreṇa tān śarān te anyonyam na avabudhyanta devānām vāhanāni ca ghoreṇa tamasā āviṣṭāḥ puruhūtasya tejasā māyā pāśaiḥ vimuktāḥ tu yatnavantaḥ sura uttamāḥ vapūṃṣi daitya siṃhānām tamaḥ bhūtāni apātayan apadhvastāḥ visaṃjñāḥ ca tamasā nīla varcasā petuḥ te dānava gaṇāḥ chinna pakṣāḥ iva adrayaḥ tadā asṛjat mahā māyām mayaḥ tām tāmasīm dahan yugānta udyota jananīm sṛṣṭā āmaurveṇa vahninā sā dadāha tatas sarvān māyā maya vikalpitā daityāḥ ca āditya vapuṣaḥ sadyas uttasthuḥ āhave māyā maurvīm samāsādya dahyamānāḥ divaukasaḥ bhejire candra viṣayam śītāṃśu salila pradam te dahyamānāḥ hi aurveṇa vahninā naṣṭa cetasaḥ śaśaṃsuḥ vajriṇam devāḥ saṃtaptāḥ śaraṇa eṣiṇaḥ saṃtate māyayā sainye hanyamāne ca dānavaiḥ purā brahmarṣi jaḥ śakra tapaḥ tepe su dāruṇam ūrvaḥ sa pūrva tejasvī sadṛśaḥ brahmaṇaḥ guṇaiḥ hiraṇyakaśipuḥ ca eva dānavaḥ dānava īśvaraḥ ṛṣim vijñāpayāmāsuḥ purā parama tejasam ṛṣi vaṃśeṣu bhagavan chinna mūlam idam padam kaumāram vratam āsthāya kleśam eva anuvartase bahūni vipra gotrāṇi munīnām bhāvitātmanām eka dehāni tiṣṭhanti viviktāni vinā prajāḥ bhavān tu tapasā śreṣṭhaḥ prajāpāta sama dyutiḥ tatra vartasva vaṃśāya vardhaya ātmānam ātmanā jagarhe tān ṛṣi gaṇān vacanam ca idam abravīt yathā ayam vihitaḥ dharaḥ munīnām śāśvataḥ purā ārṣam vai sevataḥ karma vanya mūla phala āśinaḥ janānām vṛttayaḥ tisraḥ ye gṛha āśrama vāsinaḥ asmākam tu varam vṛttiḥ vana āśrama nivāsinām abbhakṣāḥ vāyubhakṣāḥ ca dantolūkhalinaḥ tathā aśmakuṭṭāḥ hi aśanakāḥ pañca ātapa sahāḥ ca ye brahyacaryāt brāhmaṇasya brāhmaṇa tvam vidhīyate ye sthitāḥ brahmacarye tu brāhmaṇāḥ divi saṃsthitāḥ nā asti yogam vinā siddhiḥ na vā siddhim vinā yaśaḥ na asti loke yaśaḥ mūlam brahmacaryāt param tapaḥ yaḥ nigṛhya indriya grāmam bhūta grāmam ca pañcakam kva dārāḥ kva ca saṃyogaḥ kva ca bhāva viparyayaḥ nanu iyam brahmaṇā sṛṣṭā manasā mānasī prajā yadi asti tapasaḥ vīryam yuṣmākam vidita ātmanām sṛjadhvam mānasān putrān prājāpatyena karmaṇā manasā nirmitā yoniḥ ādhātavyā tapasvibhiḥ na dāra yogaḥ bījam vā vratam uktam tapasvinām vapuḥ dīpta antarātmānam etat kṛtvā manaḥ mayam dāra yogam vinā srakṣye putram ātma tanūruham evam ātmānam ātmā me dvitīyam janayiṣyati ūrvaḥ tu tapasā āviṣṭaḥ niveśya ūrum hutāśane tasya ūrum sahasā bhittvā jvālā mālī hi anindhanaḥ jagataḥ dahana ākāṅkṣī putraḥ agniḥ samapadyata didhakṣan iva lokān trīn jajñe parama kopanaḥ utpanna mātraḥ ca uvāca pitaram kṣīṇayā girā tridiva ārohibhiḥ jvālaiḥ jṛmbhamāṇaḥ diśaḥ daśa uvāca vāryatām putraḥ jagataḥ ca dayām kuru tathyam etat vacaḥ putra śṛṇu tvam vadatām vara ūrvaḥ uvāca matim etām dadāti iha parama anugrahāya vai prabhāta kāle samprāpte kāṅkṣitavye samāgame kutra ca asya nivāsaḥ syāt bhojanam vā kimātmakam yatra aham āsa niyatam piban vāri mayam haviḥ tatas yuga ante bhūtānām eṣa ca aham ca putraka sahitau vicariṣyāvaḥ niṣputrāṇām ṛṇa apahaḥ eṣaḥ agniḥ antakāle tu salila āśī mayā kṛtaḥ dahanaḥ sarva bhūtānām sa deva asura rakṣasām evam astu iti tam saḥ agniḥ saṃvṛta jvāla maṇḍalaḥ aurvasya agneḥ prabhām jñātvā svām svām gatim upāśritāḥ hiraṇyakaśipuḥ dṛṣṭvā tadā tat mahat adbhutam uccais praṇata sarva aṅgaḥ vākyam etat uvāca ha tapasā te muni śreṣṭha parituṣṭaḥ pitāmahaḥ tat mām paśya samāpannam tava eva ārādhane ratam yadi sīdet muni śreṣṭha tava eva syāt parājayaḥ tām eva māyām gṛhṇīṣva mama putreṇa nirmitām nirindhanām agni mayīm durdharṣām pāvakaiḥ api eṣā te svasya vaṃśasya vaśagā ari vinigrahe saṃrakṣati ātma pakṣam ca vipakṣam ca pradhakṣyati jagāma tridivam hṛṣṭaḥ kṛtārthaḥ dānava īśvaraḥ eṣā durviṣahā māyā devaiḥ api durāsadā aurveṇa nirmitā pūrvam pāvakena ūrva sūnunā tasmin tu vyutthite daitye nirvīryā eṣā na saṃśayaḥ śāpaḥ hi asyāḥ purā dattaḥ sṛṣṭāḥ yena eva tejasā dīyatām me sakhā śakra toya yoniḥ niśākaraḥ tena aham saha saṃgamya yādobhiḥ ca samāvṛtaḥ māyām etām haniṣyāmi tvad prasādāt na saṃśayaḥ Purāṇa evam astu iti saṃhṛṭaḥ śakraḥ cit aśavardhanaḥ saṃvideśa agratas somam yuddhāya śiśirāyudham asurāṇām vināśāya jaya artham ca divaukasām tvam mattaḥ prativīryaḥ ca jyotiṣām ca īśvareḥ caraḥ lauka chāyā mayam lakṣma tava aṅkaḥ śaśaiḥ saṃnibhaḥ svam āditya pathāt ūrdhvam jyotiṣām ca upari sthitaḥ śvetabhānuḥ hima tanu jyotiṣām adhipaḥ śaśau adhikṛta kāla dhī gatmā iṃdraḥ yajñaiḥ asaḥ avyayaḥ oṣadhīśaḥ kriyāyoniḥ ahiḥ eśasvaraṃbhā kathā śītāṃśuḥ amuta ādhāraḥ capalaḥ śvetavāhanaḥ tvam kāntiḥ kaunti vapuṣām tvam somaḥ soma pāyinām saumyaḥ tvam sarva bhūtānām timira ghaḥ tvam ukṣarāt śamaya tu āsurīm māyām yathā dṛṣṭyāma saṃyuge eṣa varṣāmi śiśiram daitya māyā apakarṣaṇam etān mad śīta nirdurdhān apaśyai tvam hima veṣṭitān daitya siṃhān mahā āhave iti uktvā tāraka adhīśaḥ sa jaleśaḥ śiva udakaiḥ prāvayāmāsa sainyāni surāṇām śānti vṛddhaye teṣām himakara utsṛṣṭaiḥ sa pāśāḥ hima vṛṣṭayaḥ veṣṭayanti sma tān dhorān daityān meṣa gaṇāḥ iva tau pāśa śītāṃśu dharaḥ varuṇa undū mahā balau jaghratuḥ hima pātaiḥ ca pāśa pātaiḥ ca dānavān tābhyām āptāvitam sainyam tat dānavam adṛśyata jagat saṃvartaka ambhodaiḥ praviṣṭaiḥ iva saṃvṛtam tau udyata ambu nāthau tu śaśāṅka varuṇau ubhau śamayāmāsatum āryām devau daitya indra nirmitām śītāṃśu jāla nirdagdhāḥ pāśaiḥ ca spanditāḥ raṇe na daityāḥ viśiraskāḥ iva adrayaḥ śītāṃśu nihatāḥ te tu daityāḥ toya hima āditāḥ nirutpannāḥ iva agrayaḥ teṣām tu divi daityānām viparīta prabhāṇi vai tān pāśa hasta grathitān chāditān śīta rasibhiḥ mayaḥ dadarśa māyāvī dānavān divi dānavaḥ sam śilā jāla vitatām khaḍgaiḥ carmaiḥ dvi hāsinīm kandara ākīrṇa kānanām siṃha vyāghra gaṇa ākīrṇām nadandiḥ gaja yūthapaiḥ īhā muga gaṇa ākīrṇām pavana ādhūrṇita dvumām nirmitām svena yatnena kujitām divi kāma gām prathitām pārvatīm māyām asṛjat samantataḥ sa asiṃśabdaiḥ śilā varṣaiḥ saṃpatan diś ca pādapaiḥ jaghāna deva saṃghān ca dānavān ca api ajīvayat naiśākarī vāruṇī ca māye antardadhatuḥ tatas asibhiḥ ca āyasa gaṇeḥ kiran deva gaṇa ātraṇe sā aśma yan crāyudha ghanā duma parvata saṃkaṭā aśmanām prahṛtāḥ kecid śilābhiḥ śakalīkṛtāḥ niṣprayatnam sura anīkam varjayitvā gadādharam sahiṣṇu tvāt jagatsvāmī na cukodha gadādharaḥ kāla jñaḥ kāla megha ābhaḥ samīkṣan kalama āhave tatas bhagavatā hṛṣṭaḥ raṇe pāvaka mārutau coditau viṣṇu vākyena tau māyām apakarpatām tābhyām udbhrānta vegābhyām pravṛddhābhyām mahā āhave dagdhā sā pārvatī māyā bhasmībhūtau nanāśa ha saḥ anilaḥ anala saṃyuktaḥ saḥ anala śvā anila ākulaḥ daitya senām dṛḍha turyuga anteṣu iva mūrchitau ceratuḥ dānava anīke krīḍantau anila analau dānavānām vimāneṣu nipatatsu samantataḥ vātaskandha apaviddheṣu kṛta karmīṇi pāvake māyā vandhe nivṛtte tu stūyamāne gadādhare saṃprahṛṣṭeṣu deveṣu sādhu sādhu iti sarvaśas jaye daśaśatākṣasya daityānām ca parājaye prakṛti stheṣu lokeṣu triṣu cāritra vandhuṣu yajamāneṣu bhūteṣu praśānteṣu ca pāpmasu yajña śobhiṣu deveṣu svagā artham darśayatsu ca lokapāleṣu sarveṣu dikṣu saṃyāna vartiṣu bhāve tapasi siddhānām abhāve pāpa karmaṇām prajā rakṣaṇa yukteṣu bhrājamāneṣu rājasu tad maiyāḥ vipulāḥ lokāḥ tābhyām kṛta jaya kriyāḥ pūrvam daitya mayam śrutvā māruta agni kṛtam mahat kālanemi iti vikhyātaḥ dānavaḥ pratyahaśyata māskara ākāra mukuṭaiḥ śikhita amaraṇa aṅgadaḥ mandarāt vipratīkāśaḥ mahārajata saṃvṛtaḥ śata praharaṇa udagraḥ śata bāhuḥ śata ānanaḥ śata śīrṣaḥ sthitaḥ śrīmān śataśṛṅgaḥ iva acalaḥ kakṣe mahati saṃvṛddhaḥ nidāghaḥ iva pāvakaḥ bhuñjakeśaḥ haricchamabhuḥ saṃdṛṣṭauṣṭuṭānanaḥ bāhubhiḥ tulyan vayoma kṣipan yan vācām mahīdharān harayat mukha niḥśvāsaiḥ vṛṣṭi yuktān valāhakān didhakṣantam iva āyāntam sarvān dva gaṇān mudhe tarjayantam sura gaṇaiḥ cādayantam diśaḥ daśa saṃvarta kāle tṛṣitam hṛdam mṛtyum iva usthitam kiṃcid calita karmaṇā tam samare dviṣatām kāla ceditam tam vīkṣante sma bhūtāni kramantam kālaneminam nārāyaṇam iva aparam saḥ ati ucchraya puras pāda māruta ādhūrṇita ambaraḥ prakāman asuraḥ yuddhe trāsayāmāsa devatāḥ samāyāntam dṛṣṭvā kālam iva aparam Purāṇa dānavānām anīkeṣu kālanemiḥ mahā asuraḥ tam trailokya antara gatam dṛṣṭvā te dānava īśvarāḥ uttasthuḥ apariśrāntāḥ pītvā amṛtam anuttamam te vīta bhaya santrāsāḥ maya tāra purogamāḥ tārakā maya saṃgrāme satatam jita kāśinaḥ rejuḥ āyodhana gatāḥ dānavāḥ yuddha kāṅkṣiṇaḥ mantram abhyasa tānteṣām vyūhan ca paridhāvatām prekṣatām ca abhavat prītiḥ dānavam kālaneminam te tu sarve bhayanti aktvā hṛṣṭāḥ yoddhum upasthitāḥ mayaḥ tāraḥ varāhaḥ ca hayagrīvaḥ ca vīryavān dānavāḥ kṛtinaḥ jagmuḥ kālanemim tam uddhatam te gadābhiḥ bhuśuṇḍībhiḥ cakraiḥ atha paraśvadhaiḥ kāla kalpaiḥ ca musalaiḥ kṣepaṇīyaiḥ ca mudgaraiḥ aśmabhiḥ ca adri sadṛśaiḥ gaṇḍaśailaiḥ ca dāruṇaiḥ paṭṭiśaiḥ bhindipālaiḥ ca parighaiḥ ca uttama āyasaiḥ ghātanībhiḥ su gurvībhiḥ śataghnībhiḥ tathā eva ca daurbhiḥ ca āyata dīptaiḥ ca prāsaiḥ pāśaiḥ ca mūrcchanaiḥ vajraiḥ praharaṇīyaiḥ ca dīvyamānaiḥ ca tomaraiḥ vikośaiḥ asibhiḥ tīkṣṇaiḥ śūlaiḥ ca śita nirmalaiḥ daityāḥ saṃdīpta manasaḥ pragṛhīta śarāsanāḥ tatas puraskṛtya tadā kālanemim mahā āhave sā dīpta śasta pravarā daityānām ruruce camūḥ dyauḥ nimīlita sarva aṅga ghanā nīla ambuda āgame devatānām api camūḥ mumude śakra pālitā upetā sita kṛṣṇābhyām tārābhyām candra sūryayoḥ vāyu vegavatī saumyā tārā gaṇa patākinī sampradīpta agni nayanā nārāyaṇa parāyaṇā sā samudra ogha sadṛśī divyā deva mahā camūḥ rarāja astravatī bhīmā yakṣa gandharva śālinī tayoḥ camvoḥ tadānīm tu babhūva sa samāgamaḥ dyāvāpṛthivyoḥ saṃyogaḥ yathā syāt yuga paryaye niścakramuḥ balābhyām tu bhīmāḥ tatra sura asurāḥ pūrvā parābhyām saṃrabdhāḥ sāgarābhyām iva ambudāḥ tābhyām balābhyām saṃdṛṣṭāḥ ceruḥ te deva dānavāḥ vanābhyām pārvatīyābhyām puṣpitābhyām yathā gajāḥ samājaghnuḥ tatas bherīḥ śaṅkhān dadhmuḥ anekaśas sa śabda udyām bhuvam khañca diśaḥ ca samapūrayat jyā ghāta tala nirghoṣaḥ dhanuṣām kūjitāni ca devāḥ tu ca aśanim ghoram parighān ca uttama āyasān nistriṃśān sasṛjuḥ saṃkhye gadā gurvīḥ ca dānavāḥ gadā nipātaiḥ bhagna aṅgāḥ bāṇaiḥ ca śakalīkṛtāḥ tatas rathaiḥ sa turagaiḥ rvimānaiḥ ca āśu gāmibhiḥ samīyuḥ te su saṃrabdhāḥ roṣāt anyonyam āhave saṃvartamānāḥ samare sandaṣṭa oṣṭha puṭa ānanāḥ ratha arathaiḥ nirudhyante pādātāḥ ca padātibhiḥ teṣām rathānām tumulaḥ sa śabdaḥ śabda vāhinām saṃhrāda māna ābharaṇāḥ jaghnuḥ tatra api carmiṇaḥ kṣarat jalānām sadṛśāḥ jaladānām samāgame deva dānava saṃkṣubdham saṅkulam yuddham ābabhau tasya vidyut cala āpīḍaiḥ pradīpta śani varṣiṇaḥ gātraiḥ nāga giri prakhyāḥ vinipetuḥ balāhakāḥ sa agni sphuliṅga pratatāḥ mukhāt niṣpetuḥ arciṣaḥ parvatāt iva niṣkrāntāḥ pañca āsyāḥ iva pannagāḥ sandhyā tapa grasta śilaḥ sākṣāt meruḥ iva acalaḥ ūru vega pramathitaiḥ śaila śrṛṅga agra pādapaiḥ apātayat deva gaṇān vajreṇa iva mahā girīn bahubhiḥ śastra nistriṃśchinna bhinna śiroruhāḥ na śekuḥ calitum devāḥ kālanemi hatāḥ yudhi muṣṭibhiḥ nihatāḥ kecid kecid tu vidalīkṛtāḥ tena vitrāsitāḥ devāḥ samare kālaneminā tena śakraḥ sahasra akṣaḥ spanditaḥ śara bandhanaiḥ airāvata gataḥ saṃkhye calitum na śaśāka ha nirjala ambhoda sadṛśaḥ nirjala arṇava sa prabhaḥ nirvyāpāraḥ kṛtaḥ tena vipāśaḥ varuṇaḥ mṛdhe raṇaḥ vaiśravaṇaḥ tena parighaiḥ kāmarūpiṇā vitta daḥ api kṛtaḥ saṃkhye nirjitaḥ kālaneminā yāmyām avasthām santyajya bhītaḥ svandiśam āviśat sa lokapālān utsārya kṛtvā teṣām ca karmma tat dikṣu sarvāsu deham svam caturdhā vidadhe tadā jahāra lakṣmīm somasya tam ca asya viṣayam mahat cālayāmāsa dīpta āśam svarga dvārāt sa bhāskaram sa ayanam ca asya viṣayam jahāra dinakarmma ca saḥ agne deva mukham dṛṣṭvā cakāra ātma mukha āśrayam vāyuḥ ca tarasā jitvā cakāra ātma vaśa anugam sa samudrān samānīya sarvāḥ ca saritaḥ balāt cakāra ātma mukhe vīryāt deha bhūtāḥ ca sindhavaḥ apaḥ sva vaśa gāḥ kṛtvā divijāḥ yāḥ ca bhūmi jāḥ stāpayāmāsa jagatīm su guptām dharaṇīdharaiḥ pāvaka anila sampātaḥ rarāja yudhi dānavaḥ pārameṣṭye sthitaḥ sthāne lokānām prabhava upame tam tuṣṭuvuḥ daitya gaṇāḥ devāḥ iva pitāmaham Purāṇa vedaḥ dharmmaḥ kṣamā satyam śrīḥ ca nārāyaṇa āśrayā sa teṣām anupasthānāt sa krodhaḥ dānava īśvaraḥ vaiṣṇavam padam anvicchan yayau nārāyaṇa antikam dānavānām vināśāya bhrāmayantam gadām śubhām sa jala ambhoda sadṛśam vidyut sadṛśa vāsasam su ārūḍham svarṇa pakṣa āḍhyam śikhinam kāśyapam khagam dṛṣṭvā daitya vināśāya raṇe svastham avasthitam dānavaḥ viṣṇum akṣobhyam babhāṣe lubdha mānasaḥ ayam sa ripuḥ asmākam pūrveṣām prāṇa nāśanaḥ anena saṃyugeṣu adya dānavāḥ bahavaḥ hatāḥ ayam sa nirghṛṇaḥ loke strī bāla nirapatrapaḥ yena dānava nārīṇām sīmanta uddharaṇam kṛtam ayam sa viṣṇuḥ devānām vaikuṇṭhaḥ ca divaikasām ayam sa nāthaḥ devānām asmākam vyathita ātmanām asya krodham samāsādya hiraṇyakaśipuḥ hataḥ asya chāyām upāśritya devāḥ makha mukhe śritāḥ ayam sa nidhane hetuḥ sarveṣām amara dviṣām yasya cakre praviṣṭāni kulāni asmākam āhave savituḥ tejasā tulyam cakram kṣipati śatruṣu ayam sa kālaḥ daityānām kāla bhūtaḥ samāsthitaḥ diṣṭyā idānīm samakṣam me viṣṇuḥ eṣa samāgataḥ adya mad bāhu niṣpiṣṭaḥ mām eva praṇamiṣyati yāsyāmi apacitim diṣṭyā pūrveṣām adya saṃyuge imam nārāyaṇam hatvā dānavānām bhaya āvaham kṣipram eva haniṣyāmi raṇe amara gaṇān tatas jāti antara gataḥ hi eṣa bādhate dānavān mṛdhe eṣaḥ anantaḥ purā bhūtvā padmanābhaḥ iti śrutaḥ dvidhā bhūtam vapuḥ kṛtvā siṃhasya arddham narasya ca pitaram me jaghāna ekaḥ hiraṇyakaśipum purā trīn lokān ujjahāra ekaḥ kramamāṇaḥ tribhiḥ kramaiḥ bhūyas tu idānīm saṃgrāme saṃprāpte tārakāmaye evam uktvā bahuvidham kṣipan nārāyaṇam raṇe kṣipyamāṇaḥ surendreṇa na cukopa gadādharaḥ aham tvām daitya paśyāmi pūrveṣām mārga gāminam prajāpati kṛtam setum bhitvā kaḥ svastimān vrajet adya tvām nāśayiṣyāmi deva vyāpāra ghātakam sveṣu sveṣu ca sthāneṣu sthāpayiṣyāmi devatāḥ evam bruvati vākyam tu mṛdhe śrīvatsa dhāriṇi jahāsa dānavaḥ krodhāt hastām ścakre saha āyudhān sa bāhu śatam udyamya sarva astra grahaṇam raṇe krodhāt dviguṇa rakta akṣaḥ viṣṇum vakṣasi atāḍayat dānavāḥ ca api samare maya tāra purogamāḥ udyata āyudha nistriṃśāḥ viṣṇum abhyadravan raṇe saṃsaktaḥ ca suparṇena kālanemī mahā asuraḥ sarva prāṇena mahatīm gadām udyamya bāhubhiḥ ghorām jvalantīm mumuce saṃrabdhaḥ garuḍa upari yadā tena suparṇasya pātitā mūrdhni sā gadā suparṇam vyathitam dṛṣṭvā kṛtam ca vapuḥ ātmanaḥ pradiśaḥ ca eva kham gām vai pūrayāmāsa keśavaḥ tarjanāya asura indrāṇām varddhamānam nabhastale ṛṣayaḥ ca eva gandharvāḥ tuṣṭuvuḥ madhusūdanam padbhyam ākramya vasudhām diśaḥ pracchādya bāhubhiḥ dīpta agni sadṛśam ghoram darśanena su darśanam advitīya praharaṇam kṣura paryanta maṇḍalam sraj dāma mālā vitatam kāma gam kāmarūpiṇam svayam svayambhuvā sṛṣṭam bhaya dam sarva vidviṣām mahā ṛṣi roṣaiḥ āviṣṭam nityam āhava darpitam kṣepaṇāt yasya muhyanti lokāḥ sa sthāṇu jaṅgamāḥ kravyādāni ca bhūtāni tṛptim yānti mahā mṛdhe samuṣṇan dānavam tejaḥ samare svena tejasā ciccheda bāhū cakreṇa śrīdharaḥ kālaneminaḥ tat ca vaktra śatam ghoram sa agni pūrṇa aṭṭahāsi vai sa chinna bāhuḥ viśirāḥ na prākampata dānavaḥ samvitatya mahāpakṣau vāyoḥ kṛtvā samañjasam urasā pātayāmāsa garuḍaḥ kālaneminam tasmin nipatite daitye devāḥ sa ṛṣi gaṇāḥ tadā sādhu sādhu iti vaikuṇṭham sametāḥ pratyapūjayan apasarpantu daityaḥ ca yuddhe dṛṣṭa parākramāḥ te sarve bāhubhiḥ vyāptāḥ na śekuḥ calitum raṇe kāṃścid keśeṣu jagrāha kāṃścid kaṇṭeṣu apīḍayan gaganāt bhraṣṭa sarva aṅgāḥ nipetuḥ dharaṇī tale teṣu daityeṣu sarveṣu hateṣu puruṣottamaḥ tasthau śakra priyam kṛtvā kṛta karmā gadādharaḥ tam deśam ājagāma āśu brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ sarvaiḥ brahmarṣibhiḥ sārddham gandharva apsarasā aṅgaṇaiḥ kṛtam deva mahat karma surāṇām śalyam uddhṛtam vadhena anena daityānām vayam ca paritoṣitāḥ yaḥ ayam tvayā hataḥ viṣṇo kālanemī mahā asuraḥ eṣa devān paribhavan lokān ca sa sura asurān ṛṣīṇām kadanam kṛtvā mām api pratigarjati tat anena tavāgyreṇa parituṣṭaḥ asmi karmaṇā yat ayam kāla kalpaḥ tu kālanemī nipātitaḥ brahmarṣayaḥ tvām tatrasthāḥ pratīkṣante sadaḥ gatāḥ kañca aham tava dāsyāmi varam varavatām vara sureṣu atha ca daityeṣu varāṇām vara daḥ bhavān asmin eva mṛdhe viṣṇo śakrāya su mahātmane devān śakra mukhān sarvān uvāca śubhayā girā viṣṇuḥ uvāca śrṛṇvantu tridaśāḥ sarve yāvantaḥ atra samāgatāḥ śravaṇa avahitaiḥ śrotraiḥ puraskṛtya purandaram svām diśam bhajatām śakraḥ diśam varuṇaḥ eva ca yāmyām yamaḥ uttarān ca dhanādhipaḥ svām diśam bhajatām śakraḥ diśam varuṇaḥ eva ca yāmyām yamaḥ uttarān ca dhanādhipaḥ ṛkṣeḥ saha yathāyogam gacchatām ca eva candramāḥ ājyabhāgāḥ pravartantām sadasyaiḥ abhipūjitāḥ hūyantām agnayaḥ vipraiḥ veda dṛṣṭena karmaṇā devāḥ ca api agnihomena svādhyāyena mahā ṛṣayaḥ vāyuḥ caratu mārga sthaḥ tridhā dīpyatu pāvakaḥ trīn tu varṇān ca lokān trīn tarpayan ca ātma jaiḥ guṇaiḥ kratavaḥ sampravartantām dīkṣaṇīyaiḥ dvijātibhiḥ dakṣiṇāḥ ca upapādyantām yājñikebhyaḥ pṛthak pṛthak gāntu sūryaḥ rasān somaḥ vāyuḥ prāṇān ca prāṇiṣu yathāvat ānupūrvyeṇa mahendra malaya udbhavāḥ trailokya mātaraḥ sarvāḥ samudram yāntu sindhavaḥ daityebhyaḥ tyajyatām bhīḥ ca śāṃnti vrajata devatāḥ sva gṛhe svarga loke vā saṃgrāme vā viśeṣataḥ viśrambhaḥ vaḥ na mantavyaḥ nityam kṣudrāḥ hi dānavāḥ saumyānām ṛju bhāvānām bhavatām ārja bandhanam evam uktvā sura gaṇān viṣṇu satya parākramaḥ jagāma brahmaṇā sārddham sva lokan tu mahā yaśāḥ etat āścaryam abhavat saṃgrāme tārakā maye dānavānām ca viṣṇoḥ ca yat māntvam paripṛṣṭavān Purāṇa ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ samāsāt bhava māhātmyam bhairavasya vidhīyatām sūtaḥ uvāca āsīt daityaḥ andhakaḥ nāma bhinnāñjana caya upamaḥ sa kadācid mahādevam pārvatyā sahitam prabhum krīḍamānam tadā dṛṣṭvā hartum devīm pracakrame tasmin yuddhe tadā rudraḥ ca andhakena atipīḍitaḥ suṣuve bāṇam ati ugra nāmnā pāśupatam hi tat andhakāḥ ca samutpannāḥ śataśas atha sahasraśas teṣām vidāryamāṇānām rudhirāt apare punar evam māyāvinam dṛṣṭvā tam ca devaḥ tadā andhakam pāna artham andhaka asrasya saḥ asṛjat mātaraḥ tadā māheśvarī tathā abrāhmī kaumārī mālinī tathā sauparṇī hi atha vāyavyā śākrī vai naiṛtī tathā saurī saumyā śivā dūtī cāmuṇḍā ca atha vāruṇī vārāhī nārasiṃhī ca vaiṣṇavī ca calat śikhā śatānandā bhagānandā picchilā bhagamālinī balā ca atibalā raktā surabhī mukhamaṇḍikā mātṛnandā sunandā ca viḍālī śakunī tathā jayā ca vijayā ca eva jayantī ca aparājitā kālī ca eva mahākālī dūtī ca eva tathā eva ca subhāgā durbhagā ca eva karālī nandinī tathā karṇamoṭī tathā grāmyā ulūkī ca ghaṭodarī kapālī vajrahastā ca piśācī rākṣasī tathā bhuśuṇḍī śāṅkarī caṇḍā lāṅgalī kuṭabhī tathā kheṭā sulocanā dhūmrā ekavīrā karālinī viśāla daṃṣṭriṇī śyāmā trijaṭī kukkuṭī tathā vaināyakī ca vaitālī unmattaḥ dumvarī tathā siddhiḥ ca lelihānā ca kekarī gardabhī tathā bhrukuṭī bahu putrī ca preta yānā viḍambinī krauñcā śailamukhī ca eva vinatā suramā danuḥ uṣāḥ rambhā menakā ca salilā citra rūpiṇī svāhā svadhā vaṣaṭkārā dhṛtiḥ jyeṣṭā kapardinī māyā vicitra rūpā ca kāma rūpā ca saṅgamā mukhevilā maṅgalā ca mahānāsā mahāmukhī kumārī rocanā bhomā sa dāhā sā mada uddhatā keśinī śaṅkinī lambā piṅgalā lohitāmukhī ghaṇṭāravā atha daṃṣṭrālā rocanā kākajaṅghikā gokarṇikā ca mukhikā mahāgrīvā mahāmukhī ulkāmukhī dhūmaśikhā kampinī parikampinī sarpakarṇī tathā ekākṣī viśokā nandinī tathā avikārā mahā citrā candrasenā manoramā adarśanā harat pāpā mātaṅgī lamba mekhalā avālā vañcanā kālī pramodā lāṅgalāvatī lamba stanī lamba saṭā visaṭā vāsa cūrṇinī skhalantī sucirā sundarī śubhā ayomukhī kaṭumukhī krodhanī ca tathā aśanī sāmānyā hāsinī lambā kovidārī samāsavī huṅkārī rudrasusaṭā rudreśī bhūtaḍāmarī piṇḍajihvā calajjvālā śivā jvālāmukhī tathā etāḥ ca anyāḥ ca deveśaḥ saḥ asṛjat mātaraḥ tadā andhakānām mahā ghorāḥ papuḥ tat rudhiram tadā tatas andhaka asṛjaḥ sarvāḥ parām tṛptim upāgatāḥ tāsu tṛptāsu saṃbhūtāḥ bhūyas eva andhaka prajāḥ arditaḥ taiḥ mahādevaḥ śūla mudgara pāṇibhiḥ tatas sa śaṅkaraḥ devaḥ tu andhakaiḥ rvyākulīkṛtaḥ jagāma śaraṇam devam vāsudevam ajam vibhum tatas tu bhagavān viṣṇuḥ sṛṣṭavān śuṣkarevatīm yā papau sakalanteṣām andhakānām asṛk kṣaṇāt tathā tathā adhikam devī saṃśuṣyati janādhipa pīyamāne tathā teṣām andhakānām tathā sṛji andhakāḥ tu kṣayat nītāḥ sarvam te tripurāriṇā cakāra vegāt śūla agre sa catuṣṭāva śaṅkaram sāmīpyam pradadau nityam gaṇeśa tvam tathā eva ca tatas mātṛ gaṇāḥ sarve śaṅkaram vākyam abruvan bhagavan bhakṣayiṣyāmaḥ sa deva asura mānuṣān śaṅkaraḥ uvāca bhavatībhiḥ prajāḥ sarvāḥ rakṣaṇīyāḥ na saṃśayaḥ tasmāt ghorān abhiprāyān manaḥ śīghram nivartyatām iti evam śaṅkareṇa uktam an ādṛtya vacaḥ tadā trailokye bhakṣyamāṇe tu tadā mātṛ gaṇena vai nṛsiṃha mūrtim deveśam pradadhyau bhagavān śivaḥ vidyut jihvam mahā daṃṣṭram sphurat kesara kaṇṭakam vajra tīkṣṇa nakham ghoram ākarṇa vyādita ānanam meru śaila pratīkāśam udaya arka sama īkṣaṇam himādri śikhara ākāram cāru daṃṣṭra ujvala ānanam nakha niḥsṛta roṣa agni jvālā kesara mālinam vajra aṅgadam su mukuṭam hāra keyūra bhūṣaṇam śroṇī sūtreṇa mahatā kāñcanena virājitam nīlotpala dala śyāmam vāsaḥ yuga vibhūṣaṇam tejasā ākrānta sakala brahmāṇḍa āgāra saṅkulam pavanam bhrāmyamāṇānām huta havya vaha arciṣām āvarta sadṛśa ākāraiḥ saṃyuktam deha loma jaiḥ sarva puṣpa vicitrān ca dārayantam mahā srajam yādṛśena eva rūpeṇa dhyāte rudreṇa dhīmatā tādṛśena eva rūpeṇa durnirīkṣyeṇa daivataiḥ praṇipatya tu deveśam tadā tuṣṭāva śaṅkaraḥ śaṅkaraḥ uvāca nakha śakti virājita andhakasya vināśāya yāḥ sṛṣṭāḥ mātaraḥ mayā an ādṛtya tu mad vākyam bhakṣayanti adyatāḥ prajāḥ kṛtvā tāḥ ca na śaktaḥ aham saṃhartum aparājita svayam kṛtvā katham tāsām vināśam abhikāraye sasarja devaḥ jihvāyāḥ tadā avāṇi īśvarīm hariḥ hṛdayāt ca tathā māyā guhyāt ca bhava mālinī asthibhyaḥ ca tathā akālī sṛṣṭā pūrvam mahātmanā yayā tat rudhiram pītam andhakānām mahātmanām yāca asmin kathitā loke nāmataḥ śuṣkarevatī dvātriṃśat mātaraḥ sṛṣṭāḥ gātrebhyaḥ cakriṇā tatas tāsām nāmāni vakṣyāmi tāni me gadataḥ śruṇu sarvāḥ tāsu mahābhāgāḥ ghaṇṭākarṇī tathā eva ca trailokya mohinī puṇyā sarva sattva vaśaṅkarī tathā ca cakrahṛdayā pañcamī vyomacāriṇī śaṅkhinī lekhinī ca eva kālasaṅkarṣaṇī tathā iti etaḥ pṛṣṭha gāḥ rājan vāgīśa anucarāḥ smṛtāḥ saṅkarṣaṇī tathā aśvatthā vījabhāvā parājitā kalyāṇī madhudaṃṣṭrī ca kamala utpala hastikā iti devī aṣṭakam rājan māyā anucaram ucyate ajitā sūkṣma hṛdayā vṛddhā veśa aśma daṃśanā nṛsiṃsa bhairavāḥ bilvāḥ garutma hṛdayā jayā bhavamālinī anucarāḥ iti aṣṭau nṛpa mātaraḥ ākarṇanī sambhaṭā ca tathā evā uttaramālikā jvālāmukhī bhīṣaṇikā kāmadhenuḥ ca bālikā tathā padmakarāḥ rājan revatī anucarāḥ smṛtāḥ aṣṭau mahā balāḥ sarvā deva gātra samudbhavāḥ trailokya sṛṣṭi saṃhāra samarthāḥ sarva devatāḥ tāḥ sṛṣṭa mātrāḥ devena kruddhāḥ mātṛ gaṇasya tu pradhāvitāḥ mahā rāja krodha visphārita īkṣaṇāḥ a viṣahya dṛṣṭi tejaḥ su dāruṇam tam eva śaraṇam prāptāḥ nṛsiṃhaḥ vākyam abravīt yathā manuṣyāḥ paśavaḥ pālayanti cirāt sutān jayanti te tathā eva āśu yathā vai devatā gaṇaḥ ye ca mām saṃsmaranti iha te ca rakṣyāḥ sadā narāḥ bali karma kariṣyanti yuṣmākam ye sadā narāḥ te ca rakṣyāḥ sat ālokāḥ rakṣitavyam mad āsanam raudrīm ca eva parām mūrtim mahādevaḥ pradāsyati mayā mātṛ gaṇaḥ sṛṣṭaḥ yaḥ ayam vigata sādhvasaḥ eṣa nityam viśāla akṣyaḥ mayā eva saha raṃsyate mayā sārddham tathā pūjām narebhyaḥ ca eva lapsyatha pṛthak su pūjitāḥ lokaiḥ sarvān kāmān pradāsyatha śuṣkām saṃpūjayiṣyanti ye ca putra arthinaḥ janāḥ teṣām putra pradā devī bhaviṣyanti na saṃśayaḥ jvālā mālā ākula vapuḥ tatra eva antaradhīyata raudrām divyām tanum tatra madhye vyavasthitaḥ sapta tāḥ mātaraḥ devyaḥ sārddha nārī naraḥ śivaḥ deveśvarasya api nṛsiṃha mūrteḥ pūjām vidhatte tripura andhaka ariḥ Purāṇa ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ vārāṇasyāḥ tu mahātmyam śrotum icchāma sāmpratam bhagavān piṅlaḥ kena gaṇa tvam samupāgataḥ annadat tvañca samprāptaḥ vārāṇasyām mahā dyutiḥ kṣetrapālaḥ katham jātaḥ priya tvañca kathaṅgataḥ sūtaḥ uvāca śrṛṇudhvam vai yathā lebhe gaṇeśa tvam sa piṅgalaḥ anna da tvam ca lokānām sthānam vārāṇasī tu iha pūrṇabhadra sutaḥ śrīmān āsīt yajñaḥ pratāpavān tadā āsīt tad namaskāraḥ tad niṣṭhaḥ tad parāyaṇaḥ āsīnaḥ ca śayānaḥ ca gacchan tiṣṭhan anuvrajan na hi yakṣa kulīnānām etat vṛttam bhavati uta guhyakāḥ bata yūyam vai svabhāvāt krūra cetasaḥ kravyādāḥ ca eva kim bhakṣāḥ hiṃsā śīlāḥ ca putraka mā evam kāṣīḥ nate vṛttiḥ evam dṛṣṭā mahātmanā āśrama antara jam karma na kuryuḥ gṛhiṇaḥ tu tat yathāvat vividhan teṣām karma tad jāti saṃśrayam mayā api vihitam paśya karma etat na atra saṃśayaḥ sūtaḥ uvāca evam uktvā sa tam putram pūrṇabhadraḥ pratāpavān vārāṇasīm samāsādya tapaḥ tepe su duścaram sthāṇu bhūtaḥ hi animiṣaḥ śuṣka kāṣṭa upala upamaḥ valmīkena samākrāntaḥ bhakṣyamāṇaḥ pipīlikaiḥ vajra sūcī mukhaiḥ tīkṣṇaiḥ vidhyamānaḥ tathā eva ca udyānam punar eva iha draṣṭram icchāmi sarvadā kṣetrasya deva māhātmyam śrotum kautūhalam hi me iti vijñāpitaḥ devaḥ śarvāṇyā parameśvaraḥ śarvaḥ pṛṣṭaḥ yathātathyam ākhyātum upacakrame nirjagāma ca deveśaḥ pārvatyā saha śaṅkaraḥ udyānam darśayāmāsa devyāḥ devaḥ pinākadhṛk virūḍha puṣpaiḥ paritas priyaṅgubhiḥ su puṣpitaiḥ kaṇṭakitaiḥ ca ketakaiḥ aśoka punnāga varaiḥ su puṣpitaiḥ dvirepha mālā ākula puṣpa sañcayaiḥ kvacid praphulla ambuja reṇu rūṣitaiḥ vihaṅgamaiḥ cāru kala praṇādibhiḥ vināditam sārasa maṇḍana ādibhiḥ pramatta dātyūha rutaiḥ ca valgubhiḥ kvacid ca kāraṇḍava nāda nāditam kvacid ca matta ali kula ākulīkṛtam mada ākulābhiḥ tu amara aṅganābhiḥ niṣevitam cāru sugandhi puṣpam pragīta vidyādhara siddha cāraṇam pravṛtta nṛtya apsarasā aṅgaṇa ākulam prahṛṣṭa nānāvidha pakṣi sevitam pramatta hārīta kula upanāditam nibiḍa nicula nīlam nīlakaṇṭā abhirāmam mada mudita vihaṅga vrāta nāda abhirāmam kusumita taru śākhā līna matta dvirepham nava kiśalaya śobhi śobhita prānta śākham kvacid ca danti kṣata cāru vīrudham kvacid latā āliṅgita cāru vṛkṣakam kvacid vilāsa alasa gāmi barhiṇam niṣevitam kim puruṣa vrajaiḥ kvacid pārāvata dhvani vikūjita cāru śrṛṅgaiḥ abhraṅkaṣaiḥ sita manohara cāru rūpaiḥ haṃsānām pakṣapāta pracalita kamala svaccha vistīrṇa toyam toyānām tīra jāta pravikaca kadalī vāṭa nṛtyat mayūram deśe deśe vikīrṇa pramudita vilasat matta hārīta vṛkṣam hṛṣṭābhiḥ kvacid api kinnara aṅganābhiḥ kṣībābhiḥ sa madhura gīta vṛkṣa khaṇḍam saṃsṛṣṭaiḥ kvacid upalipta kīrṇa puṣpaiḥ āvāsaiḥ parivṛta pādapam munīnām ramya priyaṅgu taru mañjari sakta bhṛṅgam bhṛṅga āvalīṣu skhalita ambu kadamba puṣpam gulma antara prabhṛti līna mṛga a samūham saṃmuhyatām tanubhṛtām apavarga dātṛ cāmīkara ābha nicayaiḥ atha karṇikāraiḥ phulla arivinda racitam su viśāla śākhaiḥ kvacid kāñcana saṅkāśaiḥ puṣpaiḥ ācita bhū talam sakala bhuvana bhartā lokanāthaḥ tadānīm tuhinaśikhari putryāḥ sārddham iṣṭaiḥ gaṇeśaiḥ vividha taru viśālam matta hṛṣṭa anyapuṣṭam upavana taru ramyam darśayāmāsa devyāḥ udyānam darśitam deva śobhayā parayā yutam idam guhyatamam kṣetram sadā vārāṇasī mama sarveṣām eva bhūtānām hetuḥ mokṣasya sarvadā asmin siddhāḥ sadā devi madīyam vratam āsthitāḥ nānā liṅga dharāḥ nityam mama loka abhikāṅkṣiṇaḥ nānā vṛkṣa samākīrṇe nānā vihaga kūjite kamala utpala puṣpa āḍhyaiḥ sarobhiḥ samalaṅkṛte rocate me sadā vāsaḥ yena kāryeṇa tat śṛṇu vimuktam na mayā yasmāt mokṣyate vā kadācana naimiṣe atha kurukṣetre gaṅgādvāre ca puṣkare snānāt saṃsevitāt vā api na mokṣaḥ prāpyate yatas prayāgāt api tīrthāgyrāt idam eva mahat smṛtam jaigīṣavyaḥ parām siddhim yogataḥ sa mahā tapāḥ asya kṣetrasya māhātmyāt bhaktyā ca mama bhāvanāt jaigīṣavyaḥ mahā śreṣṭaḥ yoginām sthānam iṣyate dhyāyataḥ tatra mām nityam yoga agniḥ dīpyate bhṛśam ātmanaḥ ca eva sāyujyam īpsitam sthānam eva ca kuberaḥ tu mahā yakṣaḥ tathā śarva arpita kriyaḥ iha eva ārādhya mām devi siddhim yāsyati anuttamām parāśara sutaḥ yogī ṛṣiḥ vyāsaḥ mahā tapāḥ brahmā deva ṛṣibhiḥ sārddham viṣṇuḥ vāyuḥ divākaraḥ anye api yoginaḥ siddhāḥ channa rūpāḥ mahā vratāḥ alarkaḥ ca purīm etām mad prasādat avāpsyati sa ca enām pūrvavat kṛtvā cāturvarṇya āśrama ākulām sphītām jana samākīrṇām bhaktyā ca su ciram nṛpaḥ tatas prabhṛti cāru aṅgi ye api kṣetra nivāsinaḥ gṛhiṇaḥ liṅginaḥ vā api mad bhaktāḥ mad parāyaṇāḥ iha kṣetre mṛtaḥ saḥ api saṃsāram na punar viśet ye punar nirmamāḥ dhīrāḥ satva sthāḥ vijita indriyāḥ vratinaḥ ca nirārambhāḥ sarve te mayi bhāvitāḥ dehabhaṅgam samāsādya dhīmantaḥ saṅga varjitāḥ gatāḥ eva param mokṣam prasādāt mama suvrate vārāṇasī tu bhuvanatraya sāra bhūtā ramyā sadā mama purī girirāja putri atra āgatāḥ duṣkṛta kāriṇaḥ api pāpa kṣayāt virajasaḥ pratibhānti martyāḥ asmin mṛtāḥ tanubhṛtaḥ padam āpnuvanti mūrkha āgamena rahitā api na saṃśayaḥ atra sūtaḥ uvāca dātum prasādāt yakṣāya varam bhaktāya bhāmini jagāma yakṣaḥ yatra āste kṛśaḥ dhamani santataḥ tatas tam guhyakam devī dṛṣṭipātaiḥ nirīkṣatī śveta varṇam vicarmāṇam snāyu baddha asthipañjaram devī prāha tadā devam darśayantī ca guhyakam katham evam parikleśam prāptaḥ yakṣa kumārakaḥ ruṣṭāt vā ca atha tuṣṭāt vā siddhiḥ tu ubhayatas bhavet bhoga prāpriḥ tathā rājyam ante mokṣaḥ sadāśivāt jagāma yakṣaḥ yatra āste kṛśaḥ dhamani santataḥ tam dṛṣṭvā praṇatam bhaktyā harikeśam vṛṣadhvajaḥ divyan cakṣuḥ adāt tasmai yena apaśyat sa śaṅkaram atha yakṣaḥ tadā deśāt śanais unmīlya cakṣuṣī apaśyat sa gaṇam devam vṛṣadhvajam upasthitam varam dadāmi te pūrvam trailokye darśanam tathā sāvarṇyam ca śarīrasya paśya mām vigata jvaraḥ sūtaḥ uvāca tatas sa labdhvā tu varam śarīreṇa akṣatena ca pādayoḥ praṇataḥ tasthau kṛtvā śirasi sa añjalim anna da tvam ca te lokānām gāṇapatyam tathā akṣayam avimuktam ca te sthānam paśyeyam sarvadā yathā bhaviṣyasi gaṇādhyakṣaḥ dhanadaḥ sarva pūjitaḥ ajeyaḥ ca api sarveṣām yoga aiśvaryam samāśritaḥ anna daḥ ca api lokebhyaḥ kṣetrapālaḥ bhaviṣyasi tryakṣaḥ ca daṇḍapāṇiḥ ca mahāyogī tathā eva ca sūtaḥ uvāca evam sa bhagavān tatra yakṣam kṛtvā gaṇeśvaram jagāma vāmadeva īśaḥ saha tena amara īśvaraḥ Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca imām puṇya udbhavām snigdhām kathām pāpa praṇāśinīm śrṛṇvantu ṛṣyaḥ sarve su viśuddhāḥ tapodhanāḥ māhātmyam sarva bhūtānām paramātmā maheśvaraḥ ghora rūpam samāsthāya duṣkaram deva dānavaiḥ ābhūtasaṃplavam yāvat sthāṇu bhūtaḥ maheśvaraḥ purā devena yat proktam purāṇam puṇyam uttam tatas devena tuṣṭena umāyāḥ priya kāmyayā rudrasya ardha āsana gatā meru śrṛṅge yaśasvinī mahādevam tatas devī praṇatā paripṛcchati japtam dattam hutam ca iṣṭam tapaḥ taptam kṛtam ca yat janma antara sahasreṇa yat pāpam pūrva sañcitam katham tat kṣayam āyāti tat mama ācakṣva śaṅkara śrṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi guhyānām guhyam uttamam aṣṭaṣaṣṭiḥ purā proktā sthānānām sthānam uttamam yatra sākṣāt svayam rudraḥ kṛttivāsāḥ svayam sthitaḥ japtam dattam hutam ca iṣṭam tapaḥ taptam kṛtam ca yat dhyānam adhyayanam dānam sarvam bhavati ca akṣayam janma antara sahasreṇa yat pāpam pūrva sañcitam brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyāḥ vaiśyāḥ śūdrāḥ vai varṇa saṅkarāḥ kṛmi mlecchāḥ ca ye ca anye saṅkīrṇāḥ pāpa yonayaḥ kīṭāḥ pipīlikāḥ ca eva ye ca anye mṛga pakṣiṇaḥ kālena nidhanam prāptāḥ avimukte śṛṇu priye candra arddha maulinaḥ sarve lalāṭa akṣāḥ vṛṣadhvajāḥ saḥ apibat padam āpnoti na atra karyā vicāraṇā amaram ca mahākālam tathā kāya avarohaṇam kāliñjaravanam ca eva śaṅkukarṇam sthaleśvaram avimukte varārohe trisandhyam na atra saṃśayaḥ iriścandram param guhyam guhyam āmrātakeśvaram jaleśvaram param guhyam guhyam śrīparvatam tathā mahālayam tathā guhyam kṛmicaṇḍeśvaram śubham aṣṭāv etāni sthānāni sānnidhyāt hi mama priye avimukte varārohe trisandhyam na atra saṃśayaḥ yāni sthānāni śrūyante triṣu lokeṣu suvrate atha uttarām kathām divyām avimuktasya śobhane skandaḥ vakṣyati māhātmyam ṛṣīṇām bhāvitātmanām Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca kailāsa pṛṣṭham āsīnam skandam brahma vidām varam pṛcchanti ṛṣayaḥ sarve sanaka ādyāḥ stapodhanā tathā rājarṣayaḥ sarve ye bhaktāḥ tu maheśvare mahātmā sarva bhūta ātmā devadevaḥ sanātanaḥ ghora rūpam samāsthāya duṣkaram deva dānavaiḥ ābhūtasamplavam yāvat sthāṇu bhūta sthitaḥ prabhuḥ asya kṣetrasya māhātmyam yat uktam tu īśvareṇa tu sthāna antaram pavitram ca tīrtham āyatanam tathā śmaśāna saṃsthitam veśma divyam antarhitañca yat ayuktāḥ tu na paśyanti yuktāḥ paśyanti cetasā ā deha patanāt yāvat tat kṣetram yaḥ na muñcati apāpa ātma āgatiḥ sarvā yātu uktā ca kriyāvatām yatra māsam vaset hīroḥ laghu āhāraḥ jita indriyaḥ samyak tena vratam cīrṇam divyam pāśupatam mahat janma mṛtyu bhayant tīrtvā sa yāti paramām gatim naiśreyasīm gatim puṇyām tathā yoga gatim vrajet patanāt yāvat kṣetram yaḥ na vimuñcati prāpya viśveśvaram devam na sā bhūyas abhijāyate tasya daivaḥ sadā tuṣṭaḥ sarvān kāmān prayacchati dvāram yat sāṅkhya yogānām sa tatra vasati prabhuḥ meroḥ śaktaḥ guṇān vaktum dvīpānām ca tathā eva ca samudrāṇām ca sarveṣām na avimuktasya śakyate antakāle manuṣyāṇām chidyamāneṣu marmasu vāyunā preryamāṇānām smṛtiḥ na eva upajāyate avimukte hi antakāle bhaktānām īśvaraḥ svayam karmabhiḥ preryamāṇānām karṇa jāpam prayacchati maṇikarṇyām tyajan deham gatim iṣṭām vrajet naraḥ īśvara preritaḥ yāti duṣprāpam akṛtātmabhiḥ aśāśvatam idam jñātvā mānuṣam bahu kilbiṣam vighnaiḥ ca āloḍyamānaḥ api yaḥ avimuktam na muñcati sa muñcati jarāḥ mṛtyum janma ca etat aśāśvatam avimukte prasādāt tu śiva sāyujyam āpnuyāt Purāṇa kailāsam niṣadhan ca eva meru pṛṣṭam mahā dyutim ramyam tri śikharam ca eva mānasam su mahā girim sura sthānāni mukhyāni tīrthāni āyatanāni ca teṣām anugrahaḥ kaścid tat sarvam brūhi śaṅkara śaṅkaraḥ uvāca tat sarvam sampravakṣyāmi tat me nigadataḥ śrṛṇu vārāṇasyām nadī puṇyā siddha gandharva sevitā praviṣṭā tripathā gaṅgā tasmin kṣetre mama priye mām eva prīti santuṣṭā kṛttivāsāḥ ca sundari sarveṣām ca eva sthānānām sthānam tat tu yathā adhikam kṣetrasya ca pravakṣyāmi guṇān guṇavatām vare yān śrutvā sarva pāpebhyaḥ mucyate na atra saṃśayaḥ yadi pāpaḥ yadi śaṭaḥ yadi vā dhārmikaḥ naraḥ pralaye sarva bhūtānām loke sthāvara jaṅgame na hi tyakṣyāmi tat sthānam mahā gaṇa śataiḥ vṛtaḥ yatra devāḥ sa gandharvāḥ sa yakṣa uraga rākṣasāḥ teṣām sākṣāt aham pūjām pratigṛhṇāmi pārvati dhanyāḥ praviṣṭāḥ suśroṇi mama bhaktāḥ dvijātayaḥ yaḥ mām pūjayate nityam tasya tuṣyāmi aham priye sarva dānāni yaḥ dadyāt sarva yajñeṣu dīkṣitaḥ te tiṣṭhanti iha suśroṇi tvad bhaktāḥ ca triviṣṭape avimuktam śubham prāpya mad bhaktāḥ kṛta niścayāḥ nirdhūta pāpāḥ vimalāḥ bhavanti vigata jvarāḥ pārvatī uvāca dakṣa yajñaḥ tvayā deva mat priya arthe niṣūditaḥ īśvaraḥ uvāca mahā priye mahābhāge nāśitaḥ ayam varānane avimukte yajante tu mad bhaktāḥ kṛta niścayāḥ na teṣām punarāvṛttiḥ kalpa koṭi śataiḥ api durlabhāḥ tu guṇāḥ deva avimukte tu kīrttitāḥ īśvaraḥ uvāca akṣayāḥ hi amarāḥ ca eva hi adāhāḥ ca bhavanti te avimukte mahā kṣetre aho puṇyam aho guṇāḥ īśvaraḥ uvāca māheśvari varārohe śrṛṇu tān tu mama priye avimukte tu tathā anyān api tat śṛṇu śāka parṇa āśinaḥ dāntāḥ saṃprakṣālyāḥ marīcipāḥ dantolūkhalinaḥ ca anye aśmakuṭṭāḥ tathā apare māsi māsi kuśa agreṇa jalam āsvādayanti vai vṛkṣa mūla niketāḥ ca śilā śayyāḥ tathā aparaiḥ āditya vapuṣaḥ sarve jita krodhāḥ jita indriyāḥ tri kālam api bhuñjānāḥ ye avimukta nivāsinaḥ tapaḥ caranti vā anyatra kalām na arhanti ṣoḍaśīm avimukta samam kṣetram na bhūtam na bhaviṣyati mad samaḥ puruṣaḥ nā asti tvad samā nā asti yoṣitām avimukta samam kṣetram na bhūtam na bhaviṣyati avimukte paraḥ mokṣaḥ kṣetram na eva asti tādṛśam mokṣaḥ śata sahasreṇa janmanā labhyate na vā pṛthivyām īdṛśam kṣetram na bhūtam na bhaviṣyati caturṇām api varṇānām gatiḥ tu paramā smṛtā śrutāḥ guṇāḥ te kṣetrasya iha ca anyatra ye prabho īśvaraḥ uvāca dṛṣṭyā ca eva tu mantreṇa mām eva hi yajanti te tasya aham na praṇaśyāmi sa ca me na praṇaśyati sa guṇaḥ ca eva vijñeyaḥ nirguṇaḥ manasaḥ paraḥ yā bhaktiḥ strividhā proktā bhaktānām bahudhā tvayā īśvaraḥ uvāca śrṛṇu pārvati deveśi bhaktānām bhakti vatsale prāpya sāṃkhyañca yogañca duḥkhāntañca niyacchati sadā yaḥ sevate bhikṣām tatas bhavati rañjitaḥ rañjanāt tad mayaḥ bhūtvā līyate sa tu bhaktimān śāstrāṇām tu varārohe bahu kāraṇa darśinaḥ na mām paśyanti te devi jñāna vākya vivādinaḥ parama artha jñāna tṛptāḥ yuktāḥ jānanti yoginaḥ pratyāhāreṇa śudra ātmā na anyathā cintayet ca tat tuṣṭim ca paramām prāpya yogam mokṣam param tathā tribhiḥ guṇaiḥ samāyuktaḥ jñānavān paśyati iha mām tat sarvam kathayiṣyāmi śrṛṇuṣva ekamanāḥ priye tvad rūpam kīdṛśam deva yuktāḥ paśyanti yoginaḥ śrī bhagavān uvāca amūrtam ca eva mūrttam ca jyotiḥ rūpam hi tat smṛtam guṇaiḥ viyuktaḥ bhūtātmā evam vaktum na śakyate kim pramāṇam tu tat kṣetram samantāt sarvatas diśam īśvaraḥ uvāca arddha yojana vistīrṇam tat kṣetram dakṣiṇa uttaram vārāṇasī tadīyā ca yāvat śuklanadī tu vai bhīṣmacaṇḍikam ārabhya parvata īśvaram antike gaṇāḥ yatra avatiṣṭhanti sanniyuktāḥ vināyakāḥ kūṣmāṇḍa rājaḥ śambhoḥ ca jayantaḥ ca mada utkaṭāḥ siṃha vyāghra mukhāḥ kecid vikaṭāḥ kubja vāmanāḥ yatra nandī mahākālaḥ caṇḍaghaṇṭaḥ maheśvaraḥ daṇḍacaṇḍeśvaraḥ ca eva ghaṇṭākarṇaḥ mahā balaḥ ete ca anye ca bahavaḥ gaṇāḥ ca eva gaṇeśvarāḥ mahodarāḥ mahā kāyāḥ vajra śakti dharāḥ tathā dvāre dvāre ca tiṣṭhanti śūla mudgara pāṇayaḥ suvarṇa śrṛṅgīm payasvinīm yaḥ dadyāt tri varṇām kañja locane gām dattvā tu varārohe brāhmaṇe vedapārage āsaptamam kulam tena tāritam na atra saṃśayaḥ akṣayam ca avyayam ca eva syātām tasya su locane śrṛṇu tatvena tīrthasya vibhūtim vyuṣṭim eva ca tatra snātvā mahābhāge bhavanti nirujāḥ narāḥ daśānām aśvamedhānām phalam prāpnoti mānavaḥ tat avāpnoti dharma ātmā tatra snātvā varānane vārāṇasī jāhnavībhyām saṅgame loka viśrute upavāsam tu yaḥ kṛtvā viprān santarpayan naraḥ sautrāmaṇeḥ ca yajñasya phalam prāpnoti mānavaḥ eka āhāraḥ tu yaḥ tiṣṭet māsam tatra varānane yāvat jīva kṛtam pāpam sahasā tasya naśyati agnihotra phalam dhūpe gandha dāne tathā śruṇu mālayā śata sāhasram anantam gīta vādyataḥ īśvaraḥ uvāca āsīt pūrvam varārohe brahmaṇaḥ tu śiraḥ varam pañcamam śrṛṇu suśroṇi jātam kāñcana sa prabham jvalat tat pañcamam śīrṣam jātam tasya mahātmanaḥ vāma aṃguṣṭha nakha agreṇa chinnam tasya śiraḥ mayā tadā niraparādhasya śiraḥ chinnam tvayā mama tasmāt śāpa samāyuktaḥ kapālī tvam bhaviṣyasi brahmahatyā ākulaḥ bhūtvā cara tīrthāni bhū tale tatra nārāyaṇaḥ śrīmān mayā bhikṣām prayācitaḥ tatas tena svakam pārśvam nakha agreṇa vidāritam sravataḥ mahatī dhārā tasya raktasya niḥsṛtā prayātā sā ati vistīrṇā yojana arddhaśatam tadā divyam sā ca dhārā pravāhinī provāca bhagavān viṣṇuḥ kapālam kutas īdṛśam āścarya bhūtam deveśa saṃśayaḥ hṛdi vartate śatam varṣa sahasrāṇām tapaḥ taptvā su dāruṇam jvalat tat pañcamam śīrṣam jātam tasya mahātmanaḥ śrī bhagavān uvāca tatas sarvāṇi tīrthāni puṇyāni āyatanāni ca viṣṇu prasādāt suśroṇi kapālam tat sahasradhā sphuṭitam bahudhā jātam svapna labdham dhanam yathā brahmahatyā apaham tīrtham kṣetram etat mayā kṛtam śmaśānam etat bhadram me devānām varavarṇini kālaḥ bhūtvā jagat sarvam saṃharāmi sṛjāmi ca deveśi sarva guhyānām sthānam priyataram mama sannidhānāt tu te sarve kalām na arhanti ṣoḍaśīm yatra tiṣṭhati deveśaḥ yatra tiṣṭhati śaṅkaraḥ gaṅgā tīrtha sahasrāṇām tulyā bhavati vā na vā brahma ādīnām tu te deva gatiḥ uktā sanātanī śrāvye yat dvijātīnām bhaktānām anukampayā Purāṇa jñāne vihita niṣṭānām paramānandam icchatām param guhyam samākhyātam śmaśānam iti saṃjñitam avimukte sthitaiḥ puṇyaiḥ pāṃsubhiḥ vāyunā īritaiḥ api duṣkṛta karmmāṇaḥ yāsyanti parama aṅgatim meru mandara mātraḥ api rāśiḥ pāpasya karmmaṇaḥ śmaśānam iti vikhyātam avimuktam śiva ālayam tatra brahma ādayaḥ devāḥ nārāyaṇa purogamāḥ yoginaḥ ca tathā sādhyāḥ bhagavantam sanātanam upāsante śivam muktāḥ mad bhaktāḥ mad parāyaṇāḥ yā gatiḥ jñāna tapasām yā gatiḥ yajña yājinām avimukte mṛtānām tu sā gatiḥ vihitā śubhā saṃhartāraḥ ca karttāraḥ tasmin brahma ādayaḥ surāḥ samrāj virāj mayāḥ lokāḥ jāyante hi apunarbhavāḥ brahma ādi sthāvara antasya yonau sāṅkya ādi mokṣayoḥ uttamam sarva tīrthānām sthānānām uttamañca yat kṣetrāṇām uttamam ca eva śmaśānānām tathā eva ca taṭa aṅkānām ca sarveṣām kūpānām srotasām tathā śailānām uttamam ca eva taḍāgānām tathā uttamam brahmaṇaḥ paramam sthānam brahmaṇā adhyāsitañca yat brahmaṇā sevitam nityam brahmaṇā ca eva rakṣitam puṇyāt puṇyatamam kṣetram puṇya kṛdbhiḥ niṣevitam āditya upāsanam kṛtvā viprāḥ ca amara tā aṅgatāḥ anye api ye trayaḥ varṇāḥ bhava bhaktyā samāhitāḥ aṣṭau māsān vihārasya yatīnām saṃyata ātmanām avimukte praviṣṭānām vihāraḥ tu na vidyate na dehaḥ bhavitā tatra dṛṣṭam śāstre purātane striyaḥ pativratāḥ yāḥ ca bhava bhaktāḥ samāhitāḥ anyāḥ yāḥ kāma cāriṇyaḥ striyaḥ bhoga parāyaṇāḥ sarva ātmanā tapaḥ sevyam brāhmaṇaiḥ na atra saṃśayaḥ avimuktam na sevante mūḍhāḥ ye tamasā āvṛtāḥ viṣ mūtra retasām madhye te vasanti punar punar nidrā tandrā tathā ālasyam paiśūnyam iti te daśa avimukte sthitāḥ vighnāḥ śakreṇa vihitāḥ svayam vināyaka upasargāḥ ca satatam mūrdhni tiṣṭhati param guhyam iti jñātvā tatas śāstra anudarśanāt pūtā samabhavat sarvā mahādevena rakṣitā avimuktam upāsante tad niṣṭhaḥ tad parāyaṇāḥ tam vai prāpya mahādevīm īśvara adhyuṣitam śubham avimuktam kṛtārthaḥ asmi iti ātmānam upalabhyate ṛṣi deva asura gaṇaiḥ japa homa parāyaṇaiḥ na avimukte mṛtaḥ kaścid narakam yāti kilbiṣī īśvara anugṛhītāḥ hi sarve yānti parām gatim arddha yojana vistīrṇam dakṣiṇa uttaratas smṛtam vārāṇasī tadīyā ca yāvat śuklanadī tu vai labdhvā yogam ca mokṣañca kāṅkṣantaḥ jñānam uttamam avimuktam na muñcanti tad niṣṭāḥ tad parāyaṇāḥ saritaḥ sāgarāḥ śailāḥ bhūrloke ca antarikṣe ca divi tīrthāni yāni ca ye tu dhyānam samāsādya mukta ātmānaḥ samāhitāḥ avimukte sthitāḥ nityam kṛtārthāḥ te dvijātayaḥ bhava bhaktim samāsādya ramante tu su niścitāḥ saṃhṛtya śaktitaḥ kāmān viṣayebhyaḥ bahis sthitāḥ śaktitas sarvatas muktāḥ śaktitas tapasi sthitāḥ kāraṇāni iha ca ātmānam apunarbhava bhāvitāḥ tam vai prāpya mahātmānam īśvara
