yeṣām paryyāya kāle tu bhūmiḥ āndhrān gamiṣyati Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca kāṇvāyanāḥ tatas bhūpāḥ suśarmāṇaḥ prasahyatām śuṅgānām ca eva yat śeṣam kṣapitvā tu balīyasaḥ śiśuka undhraḥ sajātīyaḥ prāpsyati imām vasundharām trayoviṃśat samāḥ rājā śiśukaḥ tu bhaviṣyati pūrṇa utsaṃgaḥ tatas rājā varṣāṇi aṣṭādaśa eva tu pañcāśatam samāḥ ṣaḍ ca śāntakarṇiḥ bhaviṣyati daśa ca aṣṭau ca varṣāṇi tasya lambodaraḥ sutaḥ daśa ca aṣṭau ca varṣāṇi medhasvātiḥ bhaviṣyati svātiḥ ca bhavitā rājā samāḥ tu aṣṭādaśa eva tu kuntalaḥ svātikarṇaḥ tu bhavitā aṣṭau samāḥ nṛpaḥ eka saṃvatsaram rājā svātivarṇaḥ bhaviṣyati bhavitā riktavarṇaḥ tu varṣāṇi pañcaviṃśati tatas saṃvatsarān pañca hālaḥ rājā bhaviṣyati pañca mandulakaḥ rājā bhaviṣyati samāḥ nṛpa cakoraḥ svātikarṇaḥ tu ṣaṣ māsān vai bhaviṣyati aṣṭāviṃśati varṣāṇi śivasvātiḥ bhaviṣyati rājā ca gautamī putraḥ hi ekaviṃśati atas nṛpaḥ aṣṭāviṃśati sutaḥ tasya sulomā vai bhaviṣyati śivaśrīḥ vai sulomāt tu saptā eva bhavitā nṛpaḥ śivaskandha śāntikarṇāt bhavitā hi ātmajaḥ samāḥ navaviṃśati varṣāṇi yajñaḥ śrī śāntikarṇikaḥ caṇḍaśrīḥ śāntikarṇaḥ tu tasya putra samādaśa sulomā sapta varṣāṇi anyaḥ teṣām bhaviṣyati teṣām varṣa śatāni syuḥ catvāri ṣaṣṭiḥ eva ca āndhrāṇām saṃsthitāḥ rājye teṣām bhṛtya anvaye nṛpāḥ sapta eva andhrāḥ bhaviṣyanti daśa ābhīrāḥ tathā nṛpāḥ sapta gardabhilāḥ ca api śakāḥ ca aṣṭādaśa eva tu yavana aṣṭau bhaviṣyanti tuṣārāḥ ca caturdaśa trayodaśa guruṃḍāḥ ca hūṇā hi ekonaviṃśātiḥ yavana aṣṭau bhaviṣyanti sapta aśīti mahīm imām bhokṣyanti imām vasundharām sapta varṣa sahasrāṇi tuṣārāṇām mahī smṛtā śatāni trīṇi aśītiṃca śatāni aṣṭādaśa eva tu śatāni arddhañcatuṣkāṇi bhavitavyāḥ trayodaśa guruṇḍāḥ vṛṣalaiḥ sārdham bhokṣyante mleccha sambhavāḥ śatāni trīṇi bhokṣyante varṣāṇi ekādaśa eva tu āndhrāḥ śrīpārvvatīyāḥ ca te dvipañcāśatam samāḥ saptaṣaṣṭiḥ tu varṣāṇi daśa ābhīrāḥ tathā eva ca teṣu utsanneṣu kālena tatas kilakilā nṛpāḥ taiḥ vimiśrāḥ janapadāḥ āryyā mlecchāḥ ca sarvaśas lubdha anṛta bruvāḥ ca eva bhavitāraḥ nṛpāḥ tathā sarve āryyāḥ mlecchāḥ ca sarvatas adhārmikāḥ caye api artham pāṣaṇḍāḥ ca eva sarvaśas praṇaṣṭe nṛpavaṃśe tu sandhyā śiṣṭe kalau yuge kiñcid śiṣṭāḥ prajāḥ tāḥ vai dharme naṣṭe aparigrahāḥ asādhavaḥ hi asatvāḥ ca vyādhi śokena pīḍitāḥ anāvṛṣṭi hatāḥ ca eva vadha īpsavaḥ aśaraṇyāḥ paritrastāḥ saṅkaṭam ghoram āśritāḥ patra mūla phala āhārāḥ cīra patra ajina ambarāḥ evam kaṣṭam anuprāptāḥ prajā kāle yuga antake niḥśeṣāḥ tu bhaviṣyanti sārddham kali yugena tu sa sandhyāṃśe su niḥ śeṣe kṛtam tu pratipatsyate evam vaṃśa kramaḥ kṛtsnaḥ kīrtitaḥ yaḥ mayā kramāt atītāḥ varttamānāḥ ca tathā eva anāgatāḥ ca ye mahāpadma abhiṣekāt tu yāvat janma parīkṣitaḥ evam varṣa sahasram tu jñeyam pañcāśat uttaram anantaram śatāni aṣṭau ṣaṭtriṃśat tu samāḥ tathā tāvat kāla antaram bhāvyam āndhra antāt ā parīkṣitaḥ bhaviṣyete prasaṅkhyātāḥ purāṇa jñaiḥ śruta ṛṣibhiḥ saptarṣayaḥ tadā prāṃśu pradīptena agninā samāḥ saptaviṃśati bhāvyānām āndhrāṇām tu yadā punar saptarṣayaḥ tu tiṣṭhanti paryyāyeṇa śatam śatam saptarṣīṇām upari e tat smṛtam vai divya saṃjñayā samā divyāḥ smṛtā ṣaṣṭiḥ divya abdāni tu saptabhiḥ saptarṣīṇām ca yau pūrvau dṛśyete hi uditau niśi tena saptarṣayaḥ jñeyāḥ yuktāḥ vyomni śatam samāḥ nakṣatrāṇām ṛṣīṇām ca yogasya etat nidarśanam saptarṣayaḥ maghā yuktāḥ kāle pārikṣite śatam brāhmaṇaḥ tu caturviṃśāḥ bhaviṣyati śatam samāḥ anṛta upahatāḥ lubdhāḥ dharmataḥ kāmataḥ arthataḥ śrauta smārta iti śithile naṣṭa varṇa āśrame tathā saṅkaram durbala ātmānaḥ pratipatsyanti mohitāḥ brāhmaṇāḥ śūdra yoni sthāḥ śūdrāḥ vai mantra yonayaḥ krameṇa eva ca dṛśyante sva varṇa antara dāyakam kṣayam eva gamiṣyanti kṣīṇa śeṣāḥ yuga kṣaye yasmin kṛṣṇaḥ divam yātaḥ tasmin eva tadā ahani pratipannam kali yugam pramāṇam tasya me śrṛṇu catuḥ varṣāṇām vai smṛtam budhaiḥ catvāri aṣṭa sahasrāṇi saṅkhyātam mānuṣeṇa tu niḥśeṣetu tadā tasmin kṛtam vai pratipatsyate ailaḥ ca ikṣvāku vaṃśaḥ ca sahadevaḥ prakīrttitāḥ ikṣvākoḥ saṃsmṛtam kṣatram sumitrā antam bhaviṣyati ailam kṣatraṃsam ākrāntam soma vaṃśa vidaḥ viduḥ ete vivasvataḥ putrāḥ kīrtitāḥ kīrti vardhanāḥ atītāḥ vartamānāḥ ca tathā eva anāgatāḥ ca ye brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyāḥ vaiśyāḥ tathā śūdrāḥ ca vai smṛtāḥ devāpiḥ pauravaḥ rājā aikṣvākaḥ yaḥ ca te mataḥ navaviṃśe yuge saḥ vai vaṃśasya ādiḥ bhaviṣyati devāpi putraḥ satyaḥ tu ailānām bhavitā nṛpaḥ evam sarveṣu vijñeyam santāna artham tu lakṣaṇam kṣīṇe kali yuge ca eva tiṣṭhanti iti kṛte yuge evam evam tu sarveṣu tiṣya anteṣu antareṣu ca saptarṣayaḥ nṛpaiḥ sārddham santāna artham yuge yuge manvantarāṇām santāne santānāḥ ca śrutau smṛtāḥ yathā praśāntiḥ teṣām vai prakṛtīnām yathā kṣayaḥ saptarṣayaḥ viduḥ teṣām dīrgha āyuḥ tvam kṣaya udayau etena krama yogena ailāḥ ikṣvākavaḥ nṛpāḥ utpadyamānāḥ tretāyām kṣīyamāṇāḥ kalau yuge jāmadagnyena rāmeṇa kṣatre niravaśeṣite dvi vaṃśa karaṇam sarvam kīrtayiṣye nibodha me ailam ca ikṣvāku vaṃśam ca prakṛtim paricakṣate rājānaḥ śreṇi baddhāḥ ca tathā ante kṣatriyāḥ bhuvi aila vaṃśāḥ tu bhūyāṃsaḥ na tathā ikṣvākavaḥ nṛpāḥ eṣām eka śatam pūrṇam kulānām abhirocate tāvat eva tu bhojānām vistārāt dviguṇam smṛtam bhojānām dviguṇām kṣatram caturddhā tat yathā tatham te hi atītāḥ sa nāmānaḥ bruvataḥ tān nibodha me śatam vai prativindhyānām śatam nāgāḥ śatam hayāḥ śatam ekam dhārtarāṣṭrāḥ hi aśītiḥ janamejayāḥ śatam vai brahmadattānām vīrāṇām kuravaḥ śatam tatas śatam ca pañcālāḥ śatam kāśi kuśa ādayaḥ tathā apare sahasre dve ye nīpāḥ śaśa bindavaḥ iṣṭavantaḥ ca te sarve sarve niyuta dakṣiṇāḥ evam rājarṣayaḥ atītāḥ śataśas atha sahasraśas teṣām te nidhana utpattau loka saṃsthitayaḥ sthitāḥ aṣṭāviṃśa samākhyātāḥ gatāḥ vaivasvate antare ā ete deva gaṇaiḥ sārddham śiṣṭāḥ ye tān nibodhita catvāriṃśat trayaḥ ca eva bhaviṣyāḥ te mahātmanaḥ avaśiṣṭāḥ yuga ākhyāḥ te tatas vaivasvataḥ hi ayam etat vaḥ rājarṣayaḥ ye tu atītāḥ te yugaiḥ saha ye te yayāti vaṃśyānām ye ca vaṃśāḥ viśām pate āyuḥ kīrtim dhanam svargam putravān ca abhijāyate dhāraṇāt śravaṇāt ca eva param svargasya dhīmataḥ Purāṇa ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ nyāyena arjanam arthānām varddhanam ca abhirakṣaṇam sūtaḥ uvāca atha atas sampravakṣyāmi mahā dāna anukīrtanam dāna dharme api yat na uktam viṣṇunā prabhaviṣṇunā sarva pāpa kṣaya karam nṛṇām duḥsvapna nāśanam yat tat ṣoḍaśadā proktam vāsudevena bhū tale puṇyam pavitram āyuṣyam sarva pāpa haram śubham pūjitam devatābhiḥ ca brahma viṣṇu śiva ādibhiḥ sarva dānānām tulā puruṣa saṃjñakam hiraṇyagarbha dānam ca brahmāṇḍam tad anantaram hiraṇya kāmadhenuḥ ca hiraṇyāśvaḥ tathā eva ca pañca lāṅgalakam tadvat dharā dānam tathā eva ca mahābhūta ghaṭaḥ tadvat ṣoḍaśam parikīrtitam sarvāṇi etāni kṛtavān purā śamvarasūdanaḥ vāsudevaḥ tu bhagavān ambarīṣaḥ atha bhārgavaḥ kārtavīryya arjunaḥ nāma prahlādaḥ pṛthuḥ eva ca kuryuḥ anye mahīpālāḥ kecid ca bharata ādayaḥ yasmāt vighna sahasreṇa mahā dānāni sarvadā eṣām anyatamam kuryyāt vāsudeva prasādataḥ mahādāna mukham kuryyāt vipraiḥ ca eva anumoditaḥ etat eva āha manave paripṛṣṭaḥ janārdanaḥ yathāvat anuvakṣyāmi śrṛṇudhvam ṛṣi sattamāḥ mahā dānāni yāni iha pavitrāṇi śubhāni ca yāni na uktāni guhyāni mahā dānāni ṣoḍaśa tāni te kathayiṣyāmi yathāvat anupūrvaśas tulāpuruṣa yāgaḥ ayam yeṣām ādau vidhīyate yuga ādiṣu parāgeṣu tathā manvantara ādiṣu saṅkrāntau tau vaidhṛti dine caturdaśī aṣṭamīṣu ca sita pañcadaśī parva dvādaśīṣu aṣṭakāsu ca yajña utsava vivāheṣu duḥsvapna adbhuta darśane dravya brāhmaṇa lābhe vā śraddhā vā yatra jāyate tīrthe vā āyatane goṣṭe kūpa ārāma saritsu ca gṛhe vā āyatane vā api taḍāge rucire tathā mahā dānāni deyāni saṃsāra bhaya bhīruṇā anityam jīvitam yasmāt vasu cā atīva cañcalam keśeṣu eva gṛhītaḥ san mṛtyunā adharmam ācaret puṇyām tithim atha āsādya kṛtvā brāhmaṇa vācanam ṣoḍaśa aratni mātram tu daśa dvādaśa vākarān maṇḍapam kārayet vidvān catur bhadrāsanam budhaḥ tad madhye toraṇam kuryyāt sāradāru mayam budhaḥ kuryyāt kuṇḍāni catvāri catur dikṣu vicakṣaṇaḥ samekhalā yoni yutāni kuryāt sampūrṇakumbhāni sahāsanāni su tāmra pātra dvaya saṃyutāni sa yajña pātrāṇi su viṣṭarāṇi hasta pramāṇāni tila ājya dhūpa puṣpa upahārāṇi su śobhanāni atra arcanam brahma śiva acyutānām tatra eva kāryyam phala mālya vastraiḥ lokeśa varṇāḥ paritas patākāḥ madhye dhvajaḥ kiṅkiṇikā yutaḥ syāt dvāreṣu kāryāṇi ca toraṇāni catvāri api kṣīra vanaspatīnām śālā iṅgudī candana devadāru śrīparṇi bilva priya kāñcana uttham stambha dvayam hasta yuga avakhātam kṛtvā dṛḍham pañcakara ucchritam ca samāna jātiḥ ca tulā avalambhyā haimena madhye puruṣeṇa yuktā dairghyeṇa sā hasta catuṣṭayam syāt pṛthu tvam asyāḥ tu daśa aṃgulāni suvarṇa paṭṭa ābharaṇā tu kāryā sā loha pāśa dvaya śrṛṅgalābhiḥ yutā suvarṇena tu ratna mālā vibhūṣitā āmālya vilepanābhyām cakram likhet vārija garbha yuktam nānā rajobhiḥ bhuvi puṣpa kīrṇam vimānakam ca upari pañca varṇam saṃsthāpayet puṣpa phala upaśobham atha ṛtvijaḥ veda vidaḥ ca kāryyāḥ su rūpa veśa anvaya śīla yuktāḥ vidhāna dakṣāḥ paṭavaḥ anukūlāḥ ye ca āryya deśa prabhavāḥ dvijendrāḥ guruḥ ca vedāntavidāryya vaṃśa samudbhavaḥ śīla kula abhirūpaḥ purāṇa śāstra abhirataḥ ati dakṣaḥ prasanna gambhīra sarasvatīkaḥ sita ambaraḥ kuṇḍala hemasūtra keyūra kaṇṭa ābharaṇa abhirāmaḥ pūrveṇa ṛgveda vidau atha āstām yajuḥ vidau dakṣiṇatas ca śastau vināyaka ādi graha lokapāla vasu aṣṭaka āditya marut gaṇānām brahmācyute sa arkam vanaspatīnām sva mantrataḥ homa catuṣṭayam syāt japyāni sūktāni tathā eva ca eṣām anukrameṇa api yathā svarūpam homa avasāne kṛta tūryya nādaḥ guruḥ gṛhītvā bali puṣpa dhūpam śubha aśubha ānanda śucām adhīśa śivāya naḥ pāhi makham namaḥ te ehi ehi rakṣaḥ gaṇa nāyakaḥ tvam sarvaiḥ tu betāla piśāca saṅghaiḥ ehi ehi yādaḥ gaṇa vāridhīnām aṅgaṇena parjanya mahā apsarobhiḥ ehi ehi yajñe mama rakṣaṇāya mṛga adhirūḍhaḥ maha siddha saṅghaiḥ prāṇa adhipaḥ kāla kaveḥ sahāyaḥ gṛhāṇa pūjām bhagavan namaḥ te ehi ehi yajñeśvara yajña rakṣām vidhatsva nakṣatra gaṇena sārddham sarva oṣadhībhiḥ pitṛbhiḥ saha eva gṛhāṇa pūjām bhagavan namaḥ te ehi ehi viśveśvara naḥ triśūla kapāla khaṭvāṅga dhareṇa sārddham lokeśa yajñeśvara yajña siddhyai gṛhāṇa pūjām bhagavan namaḥ te ehi ehi pātāla dharādhara indra nāga aṅganā kinnara gīyamāna trailokye yāni bhūtāni sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca brahma viṣṇu śivaiḥ sārddham rakṣām kurvantu tāni me deva dānava gandharvāḥ yakṣa rākṣasa pannagāḥ sarve mama adhvare rakṣām prakurvantu mudā anvitāḥ iti āvāhya surān dadyāt ṛtvigbhyaḥ hema bhūṣaṇam kuṇḍalāni ca haimāni sūtrāṇi kaṭakāni ca aṃgulīya pavitrāṇi vāsāṃsi śayanāni ca dviguṇam gurave dadyāt bhūṣaṇa ācchādanāni ca japeyuḥ śāntikādhyāyam jāpakāḥ sarvatodiśam ādau ante ca madhye ca kuryyāt brāhmaṇa vācanam tris pradakṣiṇam āvṛtya gṛhīta kusuma añjaliḥ śukla mālya ambaraḥ bhūtvā tām tulām abhimantrayet namaḥ te sarva devānām śaktiḥ tvam satyam āsthitā sākṣi bhūtā jagaddhātrī nirmitā viśvayoninā ekatas sarva satyāni tathā anṛta śatāni ca tvam tule sarva bhūtānām pramāṇam iha kīrtitā mām tolayantī saṃsārāt uddharasva namaḥ astu te yaḥ asau tatva adhipaḥ devaḥ puruṣaḥ pañcaviṃśakaḥ tvam hare tārayasva asmāt na asmāt saṃsāra kardamāt puṇya kālam samāsādya kṛtvā evam adhivāsanam punar pradakṣiṇām kṛtvā tulām ārohayet budhaḥ sa khaḍga carma kavacaḥ sarva ābharaṇa bhūṣitaḥ dharmarājam atha ādāya haimam sūryyeṇa saṃyutam karābhyām baddha muṣṭibhyām āste paśyan hareḥ mukham samāt abhyadhikam yāvat kāñcanam ca ati nirmalam puṣṭi kāmaḥ tu kurvīta bhūmi saṃstham nareśvaraḥ kṣaṇa mātram tatas sthitvā punar evam udīrayet namaḥ te sarva bhūtānām sākṣi bhūte sanātani pitāmahena devi tvam nirmitā parameṣṭinā prāpya teṣām anujñām tu tathā anyebhyaḥ api dāpayet dīna anātha viśiṣṭa ādīn pūjayet brāhmaṇaiḥ saha na ciram dhārayet gehe suvarṇam prokṣitam budhaḥ tiṣṭet bhaya āvaham yasmāt śoka vyādhi karam nṛṇām śīghram para svīkaraṇāt śreyaḥ prāpnoti mānavaḥ vimānena arka varṇena kiṅkiṇī jāla mālinā pūjyamānaḥ apsarobhiḥ ca tatas viṣṇupuram vrajet karma kṣayāt iha punarbhuvi rājarājaḥ bhūpāla mauli maṇiḥ añcita pāda pīṭhaḥ yaḥ vā śrṛṇoti paṭhati indra samāna rūpaḥ prāpnoti dhāma sa purandara deva juṣṭam Purāṇa atha atas sampravakṣyāmi mahā dānam anuttamam nāmnā hiraṇyagarbha ākhyam mahāpātaka nāśanam puṇyam dinam atha āsādya tulā puruṣa dāna vat ṛtvij maṇḍapa sambhāra bhūṣaṇa ācchādāna ādikam kuryyāt upoṣitaḥ tadvat lokeśa āvāhanam budhaḥ puṇya aha vācanam kṛtvā tadvat kṛtvā adhivāsanam brāhmaṇaiḥ ānayet kumbham tapanīya mayam śubham tri bhāga hīna vistāram ājya kṣīra abhipūritam daśa astrāṇi ca ratnāni dātrīm sūcīm tathā eva ca hema nālam sa piṭhakam bahis āditya saṃyutam tathā eva āvaraṇam nābheḥ upavītam ca kāñcanam pārśvatas sthāpayet tadvat haima daṇḍa kamaṇḍalū padma ākāram pidhānam syāt samantāt aṃgula adhikam śukla mālya ambara dharaḥ sarva ābharaṇa bhūṣitaḥ imam uccārayet mantram gṛhīta kusuma añjaliḥ brahma ādayaḥ tathā devāḥ namaḥ te viśva dhāriṇe tasmāt mām uddhara aśeṣa duḥkha saṃsāra sāgarāt evam āmantrya tad madhyam āviśya āste udak mukhaḥ muṣṭibhyām parisaṃgṛhya dharmarāja caturmukhau jānu madhye śiraḥ kṛtvā tiṣṭet ucchvāsa pañcakam garbhādhānam puṃsavanam sīmantonnayanam tathā gīta maṅgala ghoṣeṇa guruḥ utthāpayet tatas jātakarma ādikāḥ kuryuḥ kriyāḥ ṣoḍaśa ca aparāḥ caturbhiḥ kalaśaiḥ bhūyas tatas te dvija puṅgavāḥ snāpayeyuḥ prasanna āgāḥ sarva ābharayaṇa bhūṣitāḥ divyena anena vapuṣā ciram jīva sukhī bhava te pūjyāḥ sarva bhāvena bahavaḥ vā tad ājñayā grāmam vā viṣayam vā api yat anyat api sambhavet pureṣu lokapālānām pratimanvantaram vaset matim api ca janānām yaḥ dadāti priya artham vibudha pati janānām nāyakaḥ syāt amogham Purāṇa atha atas sampravakṣyāmi brahmāṇḍa vidhim uttamam yat śreṣṭham sarva dānānām mahāpātaka nāśanam puṇyam dinam atha āsādya tulā puruṣa dāna vat ṛtvij maṇḍapa sambhāra bhūṣaṇa ācchādana ādikam lokeśa āvāhanam kuryyāt adhivāsanakam tathā kuryyāt viṃśa palāt ūrdhvam ā sahasrāt ca śaktitas kalaśa dvaya saṃyuktam brahmāṇḍam kāñcanam budhaḥ diggaja aṣṭaka saṃyuktam ṣaṣ vedāṅga samanvitam śiva acyuta arka śikharam umā lakṣmī samanvitam gula śatam yāvat āyāma vistaram tathā aṣṭādaśa dhānyāni samantāt parikalpayet pūrveṇa ananta śayanam pradyumnam pūrvadakṣiṇe prakṛtim dakṣiṇe deśe saṅkarṣaṇam atas param samantāt guḍapīṭha sthān arcayet kāñcanān budhaḥ sthāpayet vastra samvītān pūrṇa kumbhān daśa eva tu daśa eva dhenavaḥ deyāḥ saha eva ambara dohanāḥ pādukā upānaha chatra cāmara āsana darpaṇaiḥ phala mālya anulepanaiḥ homa adhivāsana ante ca snāpitaḥ veda puṅgavaiḥ imam uccārayet mantram tris kṛtvā atha pradakṣiṇam saptarṣi loka amara bhū tala īśa garbheṇa sārdham vitara abhirakṣām ye duḥkhitāḥ te sukhinaḥ bhavantu prayāntu pāpāni carācarāṇām tvad dāna śastra ahata pātakānām brahmāṇḍa doṣaḥ pralayam vrajantu brahmāṇḍa dāna śakalīkṛta pātaka ogham ānandayet ca jananī kulam api aśeṣam iti paṭhati śruṇoti vā yaḥ etat sura bhavaneṣu gṛheṣu dhārmikāṇām Purāṇa mahā dānam pravakṣyāmi sarva pātaka nāśanam puṇyam dinam athā āsādya tulāpuruṣa dāna vat puṇya aha vācanam kṛtvā lokeśa āvāhanam tathā ṛtvij jhaḍapa saṃbhāra bhūṣaṇa ācchādana ādikam kāñcanam kārayet budham nānā phala samanvitam nānā vihaga vacśrāṇi bhūṣaṇāni ca kārayet śakti tāñcipalākū dhvamā sahasram prakalpayet brahma viṣṇu śiraḥ upetam pañca śākham sa bhāskaram saṃtānam pūrvatas tad vṛt turīya aṃśena kalpayet mandāram dakṣiṇe bhāge śriyām sārdham ghṛta upari paścime pāribhedam tu sāviḍyā saha jairuke surabhī saṃyutam tat valiṣṭheṣu haricandanam tūrīya aṃśena kurvīta saumyena phala saṃyutam kauśeya vajra saṃvītān ikṣu mālya phala anvitān tathā aṣṭau pūrṇa kalaśān pādukā aṃśana bhājanam phala mālya yutam tadvat upariṣṭāt vitānakam tathā aṣṭādaśa dhānyāni samantāt parikalpayet homa adhivāsana ante ca snāpitaḥ veda puṃgavaiḥ tris pradakṣiṇam āvṛtya mantram etam udīrayet viśvaṃbharāya devāya namaḥ te viśvamūrtaye yasmāt svam eva viśvātmā brahmā sthāṇuḥ divākaraḥ dhṛta armūrta param bījam atas pāhi sanātanam saṃtāna ādyaiḥ upetaḥ san pāhi saṃsāra sāgarāt catubhyaḥ ca atha ṛtvigbhyaḥ saṃtāna ādīn prakalpayet apsarobhiḥ parivṛtaḥ siddha cāraṇa kirnaraiḥ bhūtān majjyān ca manujān tārayet doṣa saṃyutān stūyamānaḥ divaḥ putra pautra śrapautrakaiḥ vimānena arka varṇaiḥ viṣṇu lokam sa gacchati divi kalpa śatam tiṣṭhet vājarājaḥ bhavet tatas nārāyaṇa bala upetaḥ nārāyaṇa parāyaṇaḥ nārāyaṇa kathā saktaḥ nārāyaṇa puram vrajet yaḥ vā paṭhet sakala kalpataru upadānam yaḥ vā śṛṇoti puruṣaḥ alpa dhanaḥ smaret vā Purāṇa atha atas sampravakṣyāmi mahā dānam anuttamam puṇyām tithim samāsādya yuga manvantara ādikīm payaḥ vratam tri rātram syāt eka rātram atha api vā lokeśa āvāhanam kuryāt tulā puruṣa dāna vat puṇya aha vācanam kuryāt homaḥ kāryaḥ tathā eva ca go sahasram bahis kuryāt vastra mālya vibhūṣaṇam suvarṇa śrṛṅga ābharaṇam raupya pāda samanvitam antar praveśya daśakam vastra mālyaiḥ ca pūjayet suvarṇa ghaṇṭikā yuktam kāṃsya dohanaka anvitam suvarṇa tilaka upetam hema paṭṭaiḥ alaṅkṛtam gavām daśaka madhye syāt kāñcanaḥ nandikeśvaraḥ kauśeya vastra samvītaḥ nānā ābharaṇa bhūṣitaḥ lavaṇat roma śikhare mālayā ikṣu phala saṃyutaḥ kuryāt pala śatāt ūrdhvam sarvam etat aśeṣatas go śate api daśa aṃśena sarvam etat samācaret puṇya kālam samāsādya gīta maṅgala niḥsvanaiḥ imam uccārayet mantram gṛhīta kusuma añjaliḥ loka adhivāsinībhyaḥ ca rohiṇībhyaḥ namaḥ namaḥ gavām aṅgeṣu tiṣṭhanti bhuvanāni ekaviṃśatiḥ brahma ādayaḥ tathā devāḥ rohiṇyaḥ pāntu mātaraḥ gāvaḥ me agratas santu gāvaḥ pṛṣṭhatas eva ca gāvaḥ śirasi me nityam gavām madhye vasāmi aham yasmāt tvam vṛṣa rūpeṇa dharmaḥ eva sanātanaḥ aṣṭamūrteḥ adhiṣṭānam atas pāhi sanātana sarva upakaraṇa upetam go yutam ca vicakṣaṇaḥ gavām ca śatam ekaikam tad arddham vā atha viṃśatim daśa pañca atha vā dadyāt anyebhyaḥ tad anujñayā payaḥ vrataḥ punar tiṣṭet eka aham go sahasra daḥ śrāvayet śṛṇuyāt vā api mahā dāna anukīrtanam tad dine brahmacārī syāt yadi icchet vipulām śriyam vimānena arka varṇena kiṅkiṇī jāla mālinā sarveṣām lokapālānām loke saṃpūjyate amaraiḥ śatam eka uttaram tadvat pitṝṇām tārayet budhaḥ mātāmahānām tadvat ca putra pautra samanvitaḥ rājarājaḥ bhavet punar aśvamedha śatam kuryāt śiva dhyāna parāyaṇaḥ saṃsāra sāgarāt asmāt yaḥ asmān santārayiṣyati anubhavati mudam vā mucyamānaḥ nikāmam Purāṇa atha atas sampravakṣyāmi kāma denu vidham param sarva kāma pradam nṛṇām mahāpātaka nāśanam lokeśa āvāhanam tadvat homaḥ kāryaḥ adhivāsanam tulā puruṣa vat kuryāt kuṇḍa maṇḍapa vedikam kanīyasī tad ardhena kāmadhenuḥ prakīrtitāḥ śaktitas tri palāt ūrdhvam aśaktaḥ api iha kārayet vedyām kṛṣṇājinam nyasya guḍa prastha samanvitam nyaset upari tām dhenum mahā ratnaiḥ alaṅkṛtām kumbha aṣṭaka samopetām nānā phala samanvitām tathā aṣṭādaśa dhānyāni samantāt parikalpayet ikṣu daṇḍa aṣṭakam tadvat nānā phala samanvitam bhājanam ca āsanam tadvat tāmra dohanakam tathā kauśeya vastra dvaya saṃyuta aṅgām dīpa ātapatra ābharaṇa abhirāmām sa cāmarām kuṇḍalinī sa ghaṇṭām suvarṇa śrṛṅgīm parirūpya pādām rasaiḥ ca sarvaiḥ paritas abhijuṣṭām haridrayā puṣpa phalaiḥ anekaiḥ snātaḥ tatas maṅgala veda ghoṣaiḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya sa puṣpa hastaḥ āvāhayet tām guruṇā ukta mantraiḥ dvijāya dadyāt atha darbha pāṇiḥ tvad dāna śastra śakalīkṛta pāpaka oghaḥ prāptaḥ asmi nirvṛttim atīva parām namāmi saṃsāra duḥkha śamanāya yatasva kāmam tvām kāmadhenum iti deva gaṇāḥ vadanti āmantrya śīla kula rūpa guṇa anvitāya viprāya yaḥ kanakadhenum imām pradadyāt prāpnoti dhāma sa purandara deva juṣṭam kanyā gaṇaiḥ parivṛtaḥ padam indumauleḥ Purāṇa atha atas saṃpravakṣyāmi hiraṇyāśva vidhim param puṇyām tithim atha āsādya kṛtvā brāhmaṇa vācanam lokeśa āvāhanam kuryāt tulā puruṣa dāna vat ṛtvij maṇḍapa sambhāra bhūṣaṇa ācchādana ādikam pādukā upānaha chatra cāmara āsana bhājanaiḥ pūrṇa kumbha aṣṭaka upetam mālya ikṣu phala saṃyutam śayyām sa upaskarām tadvat hema mārtaṇḍa saṃyutām tatas sarva oṣadhī snāna snāpitaḥ dvija puṅgavaiḥ imam uccārayet mantram gṛhīta kusuma añjaliḥ vāji rūpeṇa mām asmāt pāhi saṃsāra sāgarāt yasmāt bhāsayase lokān atas pāhi sanātana da tvā pāpa kṣayāt bhānoḥ lokam abhyeti śāśvatam sarvam śayyā ādikam da tvā bhuñjīta ā tailam eva hi purāṇa śravaṇam tadvat kārayet bhojana ādikam imam hiraṇya aśva vidhim karoti yaḥ saṃpūjyamānaḥ divi deva saṅghaiḥ yaḥ vā śrṛṇoti puruṣaḥ alpa dhanaḥ smaret vā hema aśva dānam abhinandayati iha loke Purāṇa atha atas saṃpravakṣyāmi mahā dānam anuttamam puṇyam aśvaratham nāma mahāpātaka nāśanam puṇyam dinam atha āsādya kṛtvā brāhmaṇa vācanam lokeśa āvāhanam kuryāt tulā puruṣa dāna vat ṛtvij maṇḍapa sambhāra bhūṣaṇa ācchādana ādikam kṛṣṇājine tilān kṛtvā kāñcanam sthāpayet ratham aṣṭa aśvam catur aśvam vā catur cakram sa kūbaram caturaḥ pūrṇakalaśān dhānyāni aṣṭādaśa eva tu kauśeya vastra saṃyuktam upariṣṭāt vitānakam mālya ikṣu phala saṃyuktam puruṣeṇa samanvitam ābhārāt tri palāt ūrdhvam śaktitas kārayet budhaḥ aśva aṣṭakena saṃyuktam caturbhiḥ atha vājibhiḥ puṇya kālam atha avāpya pūrvavat snāpitau dvijaiḥ tris pradakṣiṇam āvṛtya gṛhīta kusuma añjaliḥ śukla mālya ambaraḥ dadyāt imam mantram udīrayet namaḥ namaḥ pāpa vināśanāya viśvātmane veda turaṅgamāya dhāmnām adhīśāya divākarāya pāpa ogha dāva anala dehi śāntim vasu aṣṭakā āditya marut gaṇānām tvam eva dhātā paramam nidhānam dedīpyamāna vapuṣām vijita prabhāvam ākramya maṇḍalam akhaṇḍita caṇḍabhānoḥ Purāṇa atha atas saṃpravakṣyāmi hema hasti ratham śubham yasya pradānāt bhuvanam vaiṣṇavam yāti mānavaḥ puṇyām tithim atha āsādya tulā dāna vat vipra vācanakam kuryāt lokeśa āvāhanam budhaḥ ṛtvij maṇḍapa sambhāra bhūṣaṇa ācchādana ādikam kuryāt puṣparatha ākāram kāñcanam maṇi maṇḍitam kṛṣṇājine tila droṇam kṛtvā saṃsthāpayet ratham lokapāla aṣṭaka upetam brahma arka śiva saṃyutam madhye lakṣmī puṣṭi samanvitam tathā aṣṭādaśa dhānyāni bhājana āsana candanaiḥ darpaṇam pādukā anvitam dhvaje tu garuḍam kuryāt kūbara agre vināyakam nānā phala sāma āyuktam upariṣṭāt vitānakam kauśeyam amlāna kusuma anvitam caturbhiḥ kalaśaiḥ sārddham gaubhiḥ aṣṭābhiḥ anvitam caturbhiḥ hema mātaṅgaiḥ muktā dāma vibhūṣitaiḥ svarūpataḥ karibhyām ca yuktam kṛtvā nivedayet kuryāt pañca palāt ūrdhvam ābhārāt api śaktitas tathā maṅgala śabdena snāpitaḥ veda puṅgavaiḥ tris pradakṣiṇam āvṛtya gṛhīta kusuma añjaliḥ namaḥ namaḥ śaṅkara padmaja arka lokeśa vidyādhara vāsudevaiḥ vidyādhara amara muni indra gaṇa abhijuṣṭam prāpnoti asau padam atīndriyam indumauleḥ nayati sa pitṛ putrān bāndhavān api aśeṣān kṛta gaja ratha dānāt śāśvatam sadma viṣṇoḥ Purāṇa atha atas sampravakṣyāmi mahā dānam anuttamam pañcalāṃgalakam nāma mahāpātaka nāśanam puṇyām tithim atha āsādya yuga ādi grahaṇa ādikām bhūmi dānam naraḥ dadyāt pañca lāṅgalaka anvitam kharvaṭam kheṭakam vā api grāmam vā sasya śālinam nivartana śatam vā api tad ardham vā api śaktitas sāra dāru mayān kṛtvā halān pañca vicakṣaṇaḥ sarva upakaraṇaiḥ yuktān anyān pañca ca kāñcanān vṛṣān lakṣaṇa saṃyuktān daśa ca eva dhurandharān suvarṇa śrṛṅga ābharaṇān muktā lāṃgūla bhūṣaṇān rūpya pāda agra tilakān rakta kauśeya bhūṣaṇān sragdāma candana yutān śālāyām adhivāsayet palāśa samidhaḥ tadvat ājyam kṛṣṇa tilāḥ tathā tulāpuruṣa vat kuryyāt lokeśa āvāhanam budhaḥ tatas maṅgala śabdena śukla mālya ambaraḥ budhaḥ āhūya dvija dāmpatyam hemasūtra aṃgulīyakaiḥ śayyām sa upaskarām dadyāt dhenum ekām payasvinīm tathā aṣṭādaśa dhānyāni samantāt adhivāsayet tatas pradakṣiṇīkṛtya gṛhīta kusuma añjaliḥ yasmāt deva gaṇāḥ sarve sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca dhurandhara aṅge tiṣṭhanti tasmāt bhaktiḥ śive astu me yasmāt ca bhūmi dānasya kalām na arhanti ṣoḍaśīm daṇḍena sapta hastena triṃśat daṇḍam nivartanam tri bhāga hīnam gocarma mānam āha prajāpatiḥ mānena anena yaḥ dadyāt nivartana śatam budhaḥ vidhinā anena tasya āśu kṣīyate pāpa saṃhatiḥ yāvanti lāṅgalaka mārga mukhāni bhūmeḥ bhāsāṃpateḥ duhituḥ aṅgaja romakāṇi tāvanti śaṅkara pure sa samāḥ hi tiṣṭet bhūmi pradānam iha yaḥ kurute manuṣyaḥ saṃpūjyate pitṛ pitāmaha bandu yuktaḥ śambhoḥ padam vrajati ca amaranāyakaḥ san Purāṇa atha atas dharā dānam anuttamam pāpa kṣaya karam nṛṇām amaṅgalya vināśanam kārayet putrivīm haimīm jambūdvīpa aṃnu kariṇīm lokapāla aṣṭaka upetām nava vaṣe samanvitām nadīnada samopetām sapta sāgara veṣṭitām mahā ratna sabhā ākīrṇām vasuradrāka saṃyutām śata trayeṇa vā kuryāt viśatena śatena vā kuryāt pañca palāt rurdhvam aśaktaḥ api vicakṣaṇaḥ tulāpuruṣa vat kuryāt vihoktā āvāhanam budhaḥ ṛtvij jaṇḍapa saṃbhāra bhūṣaṇa ācchādana ādikam vedyām kṛṣṇa arjinam kṛtvā tilān upari vinyaset tathā aṣṭādaśa dhānyāni rasān ca lavaṇa ādikān tathā aṣṭau pūrṇa kalaśān samantāt parikalpayet vitānakam ca kauśeyam phalāni vividhāni ca tathā aśukrāni ramyāṇi śrīkhaṇḍa śakalāni ca iti evam kārayitvā tām adhivāsana pūrvakam śuk prālya ambara dharaḥ śukta ābharaṇa bhūṣitaḥ pradakṣiṇam tatas kṛtvā gṛhīta kusuma añjaliḥ puṇyam kālam atha āsādya mantrān etān udīrayet dhātrī tvam sarva bhūtānām atas pāhi vasudhare vasudharā tatas jātā tasmāt pāhi anantāryai namaḥ tasmāt pāhi saṃsāra karmamāt tvam eva lakṣmīḥ govinde śive gaurī iti ca āsthitā gāyatrī brahmaṇaḥ pārśve jyotsnā candre ravī prabhā buddhiḥ bṛhaspatī hi atā medhā muniṣu saṃsthitā viśvam vyāpya sthitā yasmāt tatas viśvaṃbharā smṛtā dhṛtiḥ sthitiḥ kṣamā kṣoṇī pruthvī vasumatī rasā etābhiḥ murtibhiḥ pāhi devi saṃsāra sāgarāt evam ucya artham tām devīm brāhmaṇyaḥ nivedayet śeṣam ca eva atha ṛtvigbhyaḥ prāṇi patya visarjayet anena vidhinā yaḥ tu dadyāt dema dharām śubhām puṇya kāle tu saṃprāpte sa padam yāti vaiṣṇavam vimānena arka varṇena kiṅkiṇī jāla mālinā nārāyaṇapuram gatvā kalpa trayam atha āvaset pitṛn putrān ca pautrān ca tārayet ekaviṃśatim iti paṭhati yaḥ ittham yaḥ śṛṇoti prasaṅgāt api kaluṣa vitānaiḥ mukta dehaḥ samantāt Purāṇa atha atas saṃpravakṣyāmi mahā dānam anuttamam viśvacakram iti khyātam mahāpātaka nāśanam tapanīyasya śuddhasya viṣuva ādiṣu kārayet tasya arddhena kaniṣṭham syāt viśvacakram udāhṛtam ṣoḍaśa aram tatas cakram bhramat nemi aṣṭaka āvṛtam nābhi padme sthitam viṣṇum yoga ārūḍham caturbhujam śaṅka cakre asya pārśve tu devi aṣṭaka samāvṛtam dvitīya āvaraṇe tadvat pūrvatas jalaśāyinam atriḥ bhṛguḥ vaśiṣṭhaḥ ca brahmā kaśyapaḥ eva ca matsyaḥ kūrmaḥ varāhaḥ ca narasiṃhaḥ atha vāmanaḥ rāmaḥ rāmaḥ ca kṛṣṇaḥ ca buddhaḥ kalkī iti ca kramāt tṛtīya āvaraṇe gaurī mātṛbhiḥ vasubhiḥ yutā caturthe dvādaśa ādityāḥ vedāḥ catvāraḥ eva ca pañcame pañcabhūtāni rudrāḥ ca ekādaśa eva tu lokapāla aṣṭakam ṣaṣṭe diś mātaṅgāḥ tathā eva ca saptame astrāṇi sarvāṇi maṅgalāni ca kārayet ṛtvij maṇḍapa sambhāra bhūṣaṇa ācchādana ādikam viśvacakram tatas kuryāt kṛṣṇājina tila upari tathā aṣṭādaśa dhānyāni rasān ca lavaṇa ādikān pūrṇa kumbha aṣṭakam ca eva vastrāṇi vividhāni ca mālya ikṣu phala ratnāni vitānam ca api kārayet tatas maṅgala śabdena snātaḥ śukla ambaraḥ gṛhī homa adhivāsana ante vai gṛhīta kusuma añjaliḥ parama ānanda rūpī tvam pāhinaḥ pāpa kardamāt hṛdi guṇa atītam viśva cakram namāmi aham anyonya ādhāra rūpeṇa praṇamāmi sthitau iha āyudhan ca api vāsaḥ ca bhavāt uddhara mām atas vaikuṇṭha lokam āsādya catur bāhuḥ sanātanaḥ praṇamet dvādaśa kṛtvā viśvacakram dine dine śubha darśana tām prayāti śatroḥ madana sudarśana tām ca kāminībhyaḥ sa sudarśana keśava anurūpaḥ kanaka sudarśana dāna dagdha pāpaḥ kṛta guru duritāni ṣoḍaśa ara pravitaraṇe pravara ākṛtiḥ murāreḥ Purāṇa atha atas sa pravakṣyāmi mahā dānam anuttamam mahākalpalatā nāma mahāpātaka nāśanam puṇyām tithim atha āsādya kṛtvā brāhmaṇa vācanam ṛtvij maṇḍapa sambhāra bhūṣaṇa ācchādana ādikam tulā puruṣa vat kuryāt lokeśa āvāhanam budhaḥ cāmīkara mayīḥ kuryāt daśa kalpalatāḥ samāḥ nānā puṣpa phala upetāḥ nānā aṃśuka vibhūṣitāḥ vidyādhara suparṇānām mithunaiḥ upaśobhitāḥ hārān āditsubhiḥ siddhaiḥ phalāni ca vihaṅgamaiḥ lokapāla anukāriṇyaḥ kartavyāḥ tāsu devatāḥ ibha āsana sthā tu guḍe pūrvatas kuliśa āyudhā rajanī saṃsthitā agnāyī śruva pāṇiḥ atha anale yāmye ca mahiṣa ārūḍhā gadinī taṇḍula upari ghṛte tu nairṛtī sthāpyā sa khaṅgā dakṣiṇa apare vāruṇe vāruṇī kṣīre jhaṣa sthā nāga pāśinī patākinī ca vāyavye mṛga sthā śarkarā upari saumyā tileṣu saṃsthāpyā śaṅkhinī nidhi saṃsthitā māheśvarī vṛṣa ārūḍhā navanīte triśūlinī maulinyaḥ vara dā tadvat kartavyāḥ bālaka anvitāḥ śaktyā pañca palāt ūrdhvam ā sahasrāt prakalpayet sarvāsām upari sthāpyam pañca varṇam vitānakam tatas maṅgala śabdena snātaḥ śukla ambaraḥ budhaḥ namaḥ namaḥ pāpa vināśinībhyaḥ brahmāṇḍa loka īśvara pālinībhyaḥ āśaṃsita ādhikya phala pradābhyaḥ digbhyaḥ tathā kalpalatā vadhūbhyaḥ Purāṇa atha atas saṃpravakṣyāmi mahā dānam anuttamam saptasāgarakam nāma sarva pāpa praṇāśanam puṇyam dinam atha āsādya kṛtvā brāhmaṇa vācanam tulā puruṣa vat kuryāt lokeśa āvāhanam budhaḥ ṛtvij maṇḍapa sambhāra bhūṣaṇa ācchādana ādikam kārayet sapta kuṇḍāni kāñcanāni vicakṣaṇaḥ prādeśa mātrāṇi tathā ratni mātrāṇi vai punar kuryāt sapta palāt ūrdhvam ā sahasrāt ca śaktitas saṃsthāpyāni ca sarvāṇi kṛṣṇājina tila upari prathamam pūrayet kuṇḍam lavaṇena vicakṣaṇaḥ dvitīyam payasā tadvat tṛtīyam sarpiṣā punar ṣaṣṭham śarkarayā tadvat saptamam tīrtha vāriṇā sthāpayet lavaṇa stham tu brahmāṇam kāñcanam śubham śarkarāyām nyaset lakṣmīm jala madhye tu pārvatīm tatas vāruṇa homa ante snāpitaḥ veda puṅgavaiḥ tris pradakṣiṇam āvṛtya mantrān etān udīrayet namaḥ vaḥ sarva sindhūnām ādhārebhyaḥ sanātanāḥ jantūnām prāṇa debhyaḥ ca samudrebhyaḥ namaḥ namaḥ kṣīra udaka ājya dadhi mādhura lāvaṇa ikṣu sāra amṛtena bhuvanatraya jīva saṅghān pāpa kṣaya amṛta vilepana bhūṣaṇāya lokasya bibhrati tat astu mama api lakṣmīḥ iti dadāti rasa amṛta saṃyutān śuciḥ avismayavān iha sāgarān Purāṇa atha atas saṃpravakṣyāmi mahā dānam anuttamam puṇyam dinam atha āsādya tulā puruṣa dāna vat lokeśa āvāhanam kṛtvā tatas dhenum prakalpayet bhūmau kṛṣṇājinam kṛtvā lavaṇa droṇa saṃyutam sthāpayet padmarāgāṇām ekāśīti mukhe budhaḥ puṣparāga śatam tadvat hoṇāyām parikalpayet lalāṭe hema tilakam muktā phala śatam dṛśoḥ bhrū yuge vidruma śatam śukrī karṇa dvaye smṛtau kāñcana anāni ca śraṅgāṇi śiraḥ vajra śata ātmakam grīvāyām netra paṭakam gomedaka śata anvitam indranīla śatam pṛṣṭe vaiḍhūrya śatapārśvake khurāḥ hema mayāḥ kāryāḥ puccham muktā āvalī mayam kuṅkumāni ca romāṇi raupya nābhi ca kārayet kuryāt śarkarayā jihvām gomayam ca guḍa ātmakam puccha agre cāmaram dadyāt samīpe tāmra dohanam kuṇḍalāni ca haimāni bhūṣaṇāni ca śaktitaḥ tathā dhānyāni sarvāṇi pādāḥ ca ikṣu mayāḥ smṛtāḥ nānā phalāni sarvāṇi pañca varṇam vitānakam evam viracanam kṛtvā tadvat homa adhivāsanam ṛtvigbhyaḥ dakṣiṇām dadyāt dhenum āmantrayet tatas guḍadhenu vat āvāhyaḥ idam ca udāharet tatas tvām sarva deva gaṇa dhāma yatas paṭhanti rudra indra sūrya kamala āsana vāsudevāḥ yaḥ puṇyam āpya dina matra kṛta upavāsaḥ pāpaiḥ vimukta tanuḥ eti padam murāreḥ Purāṇa atha atas saṃpravakṣyāmi mahā dānam anuttamam mahābhūtaghaṭam nāma mahāpātaka nāśanam puṇyām tithim atha āsādya kṛtvā brāhmaṇa vācanam ṛtvij maṇḍapa sambhāra bhūṣaṇa ācchādana ādikam tulā puruṣa vat kuryāt lokeśāvāhana ādikam kārayet kāñcanam kumbham mahā ratna ācitam budhaḥ prādeśāt aṃgula śatam yāvat kuryāt pramāṇataḥ kṣīra ājya pūritam tadvat kalpavṛkṣa samanvitam padmāsana gatān tatra brahma viṣṇu maheśvarān lokapālān mahā indrān ca sva sva vāhanam āsthitān varāheṇa uddhṛtām tadvat kuryāt pṛthvīm sa paṅkajām varuṇam ca āsana gatam kāñcanam makara upari hutāśanam meṣa gatam vāyum kṛṣṇa mṛga āsanam tathā kośa adhipam kuryāt mūṣika stham vināyakam ṛgvedasya akṣa sūtram syāt yajurvedasya paṅkajam sāmavedasya vīṇā āsyāt veṇum dakṣiṇatas nyaset purāṇa vedaḥ vara daḥ sa akṣasūtra kamaṇḍaluḥ paritas sarva dhānyāni cāmara āsana darpaṇam pādukā upānaha chatram dīpikāḥ bhūṣaṇāni ca śayyān ca jala kumbhān ca pañca varṇam vitānakam namaḥ vaḥ sarva devānām ādhārebhyaḥ carācare mahābhūta adhidevebhyaḥ śāntiḥ astu śivam mama brahmāṇḍe sarva bhūteṣu tasmāt śrīḥ akṣayā astu me ṣoḍaśa etāni yaḥ kuryāt mahā dānāni mānavaḥ iha paṭhati yaḥ ittham vāsudevasya pārśve sa suta pitṛ kalatraḥ saṃśrṛṇoti iha samyak Purāṇa idānīm kalpa nāmāni samāsāt kathaya acyuta kalpānām kīrtanam vakṣye mahāpātaka nāśanam rauravaḥ pañcamaḥ proktaḥ ṣaṣṭaḥ devaḥ iti smṛtaḥ sadyaḥ atha navamaḥ proktaḥ īśānaḥ daśamaḥ smṛtaḥ tama ekādaśaḥ proktaḥ tathā sārasvataḥ paraḥ trayodaśaḥ udānaḥ tu gāruḍaḥ atha caturdaśaḥ kaurmaḥ pañcadaśaḥ proktaḥ paurṇamāsyām ajāyata ṣoḍaśaḥ nārasiṃhaḥ tu samānaḥ tu tatas aparaḥ āgneyaḥ aṣṭādaśaḥ proktaḥ soma kalpaḥ tathā aparaḥ mānavaḥ viṃśatiḥ proktaḥ tat pumān iti ca aparaḥ vaikuṇṭhaḥ ca aparaḥ tadvat lakṣmī kalpaḥ tathā aparaḥ caturviṃśatimaḥ proktaḥ sāvitrī kalpa saṃjñakaḥ pañcaviṃśaḥ tatas ghoraḥ vārāhaḥ tu tatas aparaḥ pitṛkalpaḥ tathā ante tu yā kuhūḥ brahmaṇaḥ parā iti evam brahmaṇaḥ māsaḥ sarva pātaka nāśanaḥ ādau eva hi māhātmyam yasmin yasya vidhīyate saṅkīrṇāḥ tāmasāḥ ca eva rājasāḥ sātvikāḥ tathā rajaḥ tamaḥ mayāḥ tadvat ete triṃśat udāhṛtāḥ saṅkīrṇeṣu sarasvatyāḥ pitṛṇām vyuṣṭiḥ ucyate agneḥ śivasya māhātmyam tāmaseṣu divākare rājaseṣu ca māhātmyam adhikam brahmaṇaḥ smṛtam yasmin kalpe tu yat proktam purāṇam brahmaṇā purā sātvikeṣu adhikam tadvat viṣṇoḥ māhātmyam uttamam brāhmam pādmam imam yaḥ tu paṭet parvaṇi parvaṇi yaḥ tu dadyāt imān kṛtvā haimān parvaṇi parvaṇi brahma viṣṇu pure vāsam munibhiḥ pūjyate divi muni rūpān tatas kṛtvā dadyāt kalpān vicakṣaṇaḥ purāṇa saṃhitā ca iyam tava bhūpa mayā uditā sarva pāpa harā nityam ārogya śrī phala pradā brahma saṃvatsara śatāt eka aham śaivam ucyate śiva varṣa śatāt ekam nimeṣam vaiṣṇavam viduḥ yadā sa viṣṇuḥ jāgarti tadā idam ceṣṭate jagat yadā svapiti śānta ātmā tadā sarvam nimīlati iti uktvā devadeveśaḥ matsya rūpī janārdanaḥ paśyatām sarva bhūtānām tatra eva antaradhīyata sva antaram pālayāmāsa mārtaṇḍa kula varddhanaḥ purāṇam sarva śāsrāṇām yat etat mūrdhni saṃsthitam Purāṇa sūtaḥ uvāca etat vaḥ kathitam sarvam yat uktam viśvarūpiṇā mātsyam purāṇam akhilam dharma kāma artha sādhanam sāṃkhyam śārīrakam proktam caturmukha mukha udbhavam madanadvādaśī tadvat lokapāla abhipūjanam manvantarāṇām uddeśaḥ vainya rāja abhivarṇanam pitṛ vaṃśa anukathanam śrāddha kālaḥ tathā eva ca kīrtanam soma vaṃśasya yayāti caritam tathā kārtavīryasya mahātmyam vṛṣṇi vaṃśa anukīrtanam bhṛgu śāpaḥ tathā viṣṇoḥ ddaitya śāpaḥ tathā eva ca kīrtanam puruṣeśasya vaṃśaḥ hautāśanaḥ tathā vratam nakṣatra saṃkhyākam mārtaṇḍa śayanam tathā kṛṣṇa aṣṭamī vratam tadvat rohiṇī candra saṃjñitam taḍāga vidhi māhātmyam pādapa utsargaḥ eva ca tathā ananta tṛtīyā tu rasakalyāṇinī tathā ārdrā ānanda karī tadvat vratam sārasvatam punar uparāga abhiṣekaḥ ca saptamī snapanam punar bhīma ākhyā dvādaśī tadvat anaṅgaśayanam tathā a śūnya śayanam tadvat tathā eva aṅgārakavratam saptamī saptakam tadvat viśoka dvādaśī tathā pradānam daśadhā grahaśāntiḥ tathā eva ca graha svarūpa kathanam tathā śivacaturdaśī tathā sarva phala tyāgaḥ sūryavāravratam tathā saṃkrānti snapanam tadvat vibhūti dvādaśīvratam ṣaṣṭi vratānām māhātmyam tatā snāna vidhi kramaḥ prayāgasya tu māhātmyam sarva tīrtha anukīrtanam tata antarikṣa cāraḥ ca dhruva māhātmyam eva ca bhuvanāni sura indrāṇām tripura ādhoṣaṇam tathā pitṛ piṇḍa da māhātmyam manvantara vinirṇayam vajrāṅgasya tu saṃbhūtiḥ tāraka utpattiḥ eva ca tāraka asura māhātmyam brahma deva anukīrtanam pārvatī ṛṣi saṃvādaḥ tatā eva udvāha maṅgalam vārāṇasyāḥ tu māhātmyam narmadāyāḥ tathā eva ca tatas ubhaya mukhī dānam dānam kṛṣṇājinasya ca tathā sāvitrī upākhyānam rāja dharmāḥ tathā eva ca vāmanasya tu māhātmyam tathā eva atha varāha jam kṣīroda mathanam tadvat kālakūṭa abhiśāsanam prāsāda lakṣaṇam tadvat maṇḍapānām tu lakṣaṇam kalpa anukīrtanam tadvat grantha anukramaṇīm tathā tulā dāna ādi bahuśas mahā dāna anukīrtanam kalpa anukīrtanam tadvat granta anukramaṇīm tathā etat pavitram kalyāṇam mahā pāpa haram śubham asmāt purāṇāt api pādam ekam paṭet tu yaḥ saḥ api vimukta pāpaḥ nārāyaṇa ākhyam padam eti nūnam anaṅga vat divya sukhāni bhuṅkte samāptam idam śrī matsya mahā purāṇam
